The working thread

Friday 25 September 2026, 18:03 (PDT), for 1 h 14 min. 202 steps, 106 images looked at.

This is the student's own thread: the brief it was given, what it wrote, each step it took, and every image it opened, shown as it saw it (possibly downscaled). Some of these images were later overwritten in the study folder, so this is the only place they survive. The raw record is the transcript.

The brief

Learn to paint like Mark Ferrari, the great painterly pixel artist, by first learning to see the real world the way a landscape painter does. Your task is in ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/shared/TASK.md, with his tarn painting beside it.

Method of study: observation of nature first, then translation.

  1. Gather and study photographs of the real things: weathered granite boulders in grass at mountain lakes, grass banks and sedge at water's edges, still water reflecting, all in low warm evening light and other lights. Save the best ones to refs/ (Wikimedia Commons is a good source). Look at them yourself with the Read tool.
  2. Observe and write down what's actually there: - How light wraps a rounded boulder: light side, core shadow, bounce light from the grass, the cast shadow and the dark contact line where it meets the ground. - How grass masses turn in space and catch light at their tops and edges. - How the bank's lip meets the water, and how reflections darken and simplify. - How distance changes all of it. Make simple observational studies with a program: greyscale block-ins of the photos' big shapes and values.
  3. Study how Ferrari translates exactly these observations into pixels: what he keeps, what he simplifies, which marks stand for which truths.
  4. Construct a method: a program that paints the task's subject from parameters, carrying real observed form and light into Ferrari's language. Output the parameter sheets the task asks for.

Write LESSONS.md: the photos and your observations, the studies, the translation from nature to Ferrari, the method, and an honest comparison of your sheets against both the photos and the reference.

Rules: work only in ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation/ (read shared/). Don't read or search anywhere else on this disk. Run programs with nice -n 10, at most 4 threads. Don't worry about performance. Take the time mastery takes.

# The task (identical for every study)

Paint, as painterly pixel art in the manner of Mark Ferrari (see ferrari_alpine_tarn_dusk.png beside this file):

**A granite boulder resting in a grass bank at the edge of a still mountain tarn, at dusk.**

- Boulder: 1–3 m, rounded by weathering, partly sunk in the bank. Grass and sedge grow up around its foot.
- The bank slopes gently down to the water's edge. The water reflects the boulder, bank and sky.
- Light: low sun from the left, warm; the sky is pink to violet.
- Frame: 320×180 native pixels, shown upscaled 3× with nearest neighbour. Palette of at most 32 colours, chosen deliberately.
- Camera: a low angle, about 10° down, a few metres away.

The deliverable is a METHOD, not one image. Write a program that paints this subject from parameters, and output SHEETS that sweep them:

- boulder size (small / medium / large);
- light direction (left, front, right, back-lit);
- distance (close, 10 m, 40 m);
- random seed.

Every cell should look painted.
Read: pedagogy/shared/ferrari_alpine_tarn_dusk.png

1. pedagogy/shared/ferrari_alpine_tarn_dusk.png

Bash: Download candidate reference photos from Commons
mkdir -p ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation/refs/cand && cat > ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation/fetch_refs.py <<'EOF'
# Search Wikimedia Commons and download 800px thumbnails of candidate reference photos.
import json, sys, os, re, urllib.request, urllib.parse, time
UA = {"User-Agent": "PixelStudy/1.0 (painting study; contact via commons)"}
OUT = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "refs", "cand")
def get(url):
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=UA)
    return urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60).read()
def search(q, n=12):
    p = dict(action="query", generator="search", gsrsearch=q + " filetype:bitmap", gsrnamespace=6,
             gsrlimit=n, prop="imageinfo", iiprop="url|extmetadata", iiurlwidth=800, format="json")
    d = json.loads(get("https://commons.wikimedia.org/w/api.php?" + urllib.parse.urlencode(p)))
    return list(d.get("query", {}).get("pages", {}).values())
if __name__ == "__main__":
    tag = sys.argv[1]; q = " ".join(sys.argv[2:])
    for i, pg in enumerate(search(q)):
        ii = pg["imageinfo"][0]; url = ii.get("thumburl") or ii["url"]
        name = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]+", "_", pg["title"][5:])[:60]
        fn = os.path.join(OUT, f"{tag}_{i:02d}_{name}.jpg")
        if os.path.exists(fn): continue
        try:
            open(fn, "wb").write(get(url)); print(fn, pg["title"])
            lic = ii.get("extmetadata", {}).get("LicenseShortName", {}).get("value", "?")
            art = re.sub("<[^>]+>", "", ii.get("extmetadata", {}).get("Artist", {}).get("value", "?"))
            with open(os.path.join(OUT, "credits.tsv"), "a") as f: f.write(f"{os.path.basename(fn)}\t{pg['title']}\t{art.strip()}\t{lic}\t{ii['descriptionurl']}\n")
        except Exception as e: print("ERR", e)
        time.sleep(0.5)
EOF
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && for q in "boulder:glacial erratic boulder grass" "tarn:alpine tarn boulder" "bldsun:boulder evening light meadow" "granite:granite boulder meadow" "lakeshore:mountain lake shore grass reflection" "sedge:sedge lake shore" "dusk:mountain lake dusk reflection" "sierra:Sierra Nevada lake granite boulders sunset"; do nice -n 10 python3 fetch_refs.py ${q%%:*} ${q#*:} 2>&1 | tail -3; done; ls refs/cand | wc -l

Bash: Fetch more candidates and build contact sheet
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && for q in "rock:rock lake shore golden hour" "erratic:erratic boulder sunset" "boulders2:boulder field alpine meadow evening" "still:still lake reflection mountain evening" "grassbank:grassy bank pond reflection" "lakegrass:lake shore grass sunset" "bldlake:boulder in lake reflection" "alpen:Bergsee Felsblock Abendlicht" "sunrisebld:boulder sunrise light" "backlit:backlit grass sunset" "ponds:tarn Lake District evening" "yosemite:Tuolumne Meadows boulder sunset"; do nice -n 10 python3 fetch_refs.py ${q%%:*} ${q#*:} >/dev/null 2>&1; done; ls refs/cand/*.jpg | wc -l
cat > contact.py <<'EOF'
import sys, glob, os
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
files = sorted(glob.glob(sys.argv[1])); out = sys.argv[2]; W = 256; cols = 6
rows = (len(files)+cols-1)//cols; sheet = Image.new("RGB", (cols*W, rows*(W+14)), "white"); d = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
for i, f in enumerate(files):
    try: im = Image.open(f).convert("RGB")
    except Exception: continue
    im.thumbnail((W, W)); x, y = (i%cols)*W, (i//cols)*(W+14)
    sheet.paste(im, (x, y)); d.text((x+2, y+W), f"{i} {os.path.basename(f)[:38]}", fill="black")
sheet.save(out); print(len(files))
EOF
python3 contact.py 'refs/cand/*.jpg' studies/contact_all.png

Bash: Add retry/backoff and refetch
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s=open('fetch_refs.py').read()
s=s.replace('iiurlwidth=800','iiurlwidth=960').replace('time.sleep(0.5)','time.sleep(2.5)')
s=s.replace('''def get(url):
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=UA)
    return urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60).read()''','''def get(url, tries=4):
    for t in range(tries):
        try:
            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=UA)
            return urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60).read()
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            if e.code != 429 or t == tries-1: raise
            time.sleep(15*(t+1))''')
s=s.replace('import json, sys, os, re, urllib.request','import json, sys, os, re, urllib.request, urllib.error')
open('fetch_refs.py','w').write(s)
EOF

Bash: Measure grass stroke run lengths by depth
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 -c "
from PIL import Image; import numpy as np
a=np.array(Image.open('ferrari/native.png').convert('RGB')).astype(int)
H,W,_=a.shape
def runs(reg,axis):
  if axis==0: reg=reg.transpose(1,0,2)
  L=[]
  for r in reg:
    ch=np.where(np.any(r[1:]!=r[:-1],axis=1))[0]; L+=list(np.diff(ch))
  return np.mean(L) if L else 0
for y0 in range(220,469,20):
  reg=a[y0:y0+20,0:110]; regR=a[y0:y0+20,520:634]
  print(y0,'L vert %.2f horiz %.2f'%(runs(reg,0),runs(reg,1)),' R vert %.2f horiz %.2f'%(runs(regR,0),runs(regR,1)), 'ncol',len(np.unique(reg.reshape(-1,3),axis=0)))

Bash: Write and run greyscale block-in study program
mkdir -p ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation/studies && cat > ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation/blockin.py <<'EOF'
"""Observational study: greyscale block-in of a photo's big shapes and values.
photo -> luminance -> edge-preserving simplification (SLIC superpixels, mean value)
-> quantize to N value steps (k-means in luminance) -> side-by-side with the photo.
This is the squint: what are the 3-5 big value masses, and where are the edges between them?"""
import sys, os, numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from skimage.segmentation import slic
from skimage.color import rgb2lab
from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
os.environ.setdefault("OMP_NUM_THREADS", "4")

def blockin(path, nvals=4, nseg=180, W=480):
    im = Image.open(path).convert("RGB"); im.thumbnail((W, W))
    a = np.asarray(im) / 255.0
    L = rgb2lab(a)[..., 0] / 100.0
    seg = slic(a, n_segments=nseg, compactness=12, start_label=0)
    Ls = np.zeros_like(L)
    for s in np.unique(seg): Ls[seg == s] = L[seg == s].mean()
    km = KMeans(nvals, n_init=4, random_state=0).fit(Ls.reshape(-1, 1))
    cent = np.sort(km.cluster_centers_.ravel()); lab = np.argmin(np.abs(Ls[..., None] - cent), -1)
    grey = np.linspace(0.08, 0.95, nvals)[lab]
    return im, (L * 255).astype(np.uint8), (grey * 255).astype(np.uint8), cent

if __name__ == "__main__":
    out = sys.argv[1]; paths = sys.argv[2:]; rows = []
    for p in paths:
        im, L, g, cent = blockin(p)
        w, h = im.size; row = Image.new("RGB", (w*3+8, h+16), "white")
        row.paste(im, (0, 0)); row.paste(Image.fromarray(L).convert("RGB"), (w+4, 0)); row.paste(Image.fromarray(g).convert("RGB"), (2*w+8, 0))
        ImageDraw.Draw(row).text((2, h+2), os.path.basename(p) + "  value centres L*=" + " ".join(f"{c*100:.0f}" for c in cent), fill="black")
        rows.append(row)
    W = max(r.size[0] for r in rows); H = sum(r.size[1] for r in rows)
    sheet = Image.new("RGB", (W, H), "white"); y = 0
    for r in rows: sheet.paste(r, (0, y)); y += r.size[1]
    sheet.save(out)
EOF

Bash: Rewrite block-in as notan plus squint quantization
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && cat > blockin.py <<'EOF'
"""Observational study: greyscale block-in of a photo's big shapes and values.
The squint, done by program:
  panel 1  the photo
  panel 2  2-value notan: light family vs shadow family (Otsu split of blurred luminance)
  panel 3  4-value block-in at 80 px wide (median-filtered, multi-Otsu), shown nearest-neighbour
  panel 4  the same 4 values, but at 320 px wide - what survives at the task's native resolution
Printed: the L* centre of each value mass, so observations carry numbers."""
import sys, os, numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw, ImageFilter
from skimage.color import rgb2lab
from skimage.filters import threshold_multiotsu, threshold_otsu

def lum(im): return rgb2lab(np.asarray(im) / 255.0)[..., 0]

def quant(L, k):
    t = threshold_multiotsu(L, classes=k); q = np.digitize(L, t)
    cent = [L[q == i].mean() for i in range(k)]
    return q, cent

def panels(path, W=400):
    im = Image.open(path).convert("RGB"); im.thumbnail((W, W)); w, h = im.size
    L = lum(im.filter(ImageFilter.GaussianBlur(w / 120)))
    t = threshold_otsu(L); notan = np.where(L > t, 230, 40).astype(np.uint8)
    out = [im, Image.fromarray(notan).convert("RGB")]
    for sw in (80, 320 * w // W if False else 160):
        small = im.resize((sw, max(1, sw * h // w)), Image.LANCZOS).filter(ImageFilter.MedianFilter(3))
        Ls = lum(small); q, cent = quant(Ls, 4)
        grey = np.array([30, 95, 165, 235], np.uint8)[q]
        out.append(Image.fromarray(grey).convert("RGB").resize((w, h), Image.NEAREST))
    return out, cent, t

if __name__ == "__main__":
    dst = sys.argv[1]; rows = []
    for p in sys.argv[2:]:
        ps, cent, t = panels(p); w, h = ps[0].size
        row = Image.new("RGB", (4 * w + 12, h + 14), "white")
        for i, q in enumerate(ps): row.paste(q, (i * (w + 4), 0))
        ImageDraw.Draw(row).text((2, h + 1), f"{os.path.basename(p)}   light/shadow split L*={t:.0f}   4-value centres L*=" + " ".join(f"{c:.0f}" for c in cent), fill="black")
        rows.append(row)
    Wd = max(r.size[0] for r in rows); sheet = Image.new("RGB", (Wd, sum(r.size[1] for r in rows)), "white"); y = 0
    for r in rows: sheet.paste(r, (0, y)); y += r.size[1]
    sheet.save(dst)
EOF

0 0 4 1 0 0 0 0 1 2 1      121  [exited with code 0] 1.6.1 1.14.0

Write: pedagogy/observation/tarn.py
"""
tarn.py -- paint "a granite boulder in a grass bank at the edge of a still mountain tarn, at dusk"
as painterly pixel art, from parameters.

Two stages, mirroring the study method:

  1. OBSERVE  (render_observation)  a small physical model of the scene, carrying what the photos
     showed: low warm sun + violet sky fill + warm bounce from lit grass; a rough-surface terminator
     (flat light plateau, sharp drop, core-shadow dip before the bounce-lit shadow side); contact
     occlusion; long cast shadows at 5 deg sun; tussock-scale grass masses that catch light on
     their sun-facing tops; water as a Fresnel mirror (reflections darker, showing the UNDERSIDE of
     things, darkening toward the viewer); aerial perspective on distant land.
     Output: a per-pixel G-buffer and a continuous colour image at 320x180.

  2. TRANSLATE (paint)  that observation into Ferrari's language:
     - a 32-colour palette built from the model's own canonical light states (ramps per material)
     - sky: flat bands of adjacent ramp colours joined by 50% checkerboard strips
     - grass: stipple in the distance, vertical 1-px blades near, blade length ~ 1/distance,
       blades dark at the root and light at the tip, dark "gap" blades interleaved, painted far to
       near so near tips overlap the water and the boulder's foot
     - boulder: few values, sharp light/shadow split, dither only across the terminator, a 1-px
       rim where the silhouette faces the sun, darkest accent at the contact line, sparse speckle
     - water: the same scene mirrored and darkened, broken into horizontal dashes by
       horizontally-correlated ripple noise, light wind-lines, a light line at the far shore
     - far trees and mountains: flat facets, lit edges, heavy aerial perspective

Parameters: size (small|medium|large), light (left|front|right|back), distance (close|10m|40m), seed.
"""
import os, sys, math, argparse
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from skimage.color import rgb2lab, lab2rgb

W, H = 320, 180
HFOV = math.radians(60.0)
PITCH = math.radians(-10.0)
EYE_H = 1.25                         # camera eye height above its local ground (m) - "low angle"
SUN_EL = math.radians(4.5)           # dusk: sun just above the ridge line
SIZES = {"small": 1.1, "medium": 1.9, "large": 2.9}                 # boulder long axis, m
DISTS = {"close": 5.5, "10m": 10.0, "40m": 40.0}                   # camera -> boulder, m
LIGHTS = {"left": -80.0, "front": 172.0, "right": 80.0, "back": -12.0}  # sun azimuth rel. to view, deg

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# noise
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
M32 = np.uint64(0xFFFFFFFF)

def _hash(ix, iy, iz, seed):
    h = (ix.astype(np.int64).astype(np.uint64) * np.uint64(73856093)) ^ \
        (iy.astype(np.int64).astype(np.uint64) * np.uint64(19349663)) ^ \
        (iz.astype(np.int64).astype(np.uint64) * np.uint64(83492791)) ^ np.uint64(seed * 2654435761 & 0xFFFFFFFF)
    h &= M32
    h ^= h >> np.uint64(13); h = (h * np.uint64(1274126177)) & M32; h ^= h >> np.uint64(16)
    return (h & np.uint64(0xFFFFFF)).astype(np.float64) / float(0xFFFFFF)

def vnoise3(x, y, z, seed=0):
    ix, iy, iz = np.floor(x), np.floor(y), np.floor(z)
    fx, fy, fz = x - ix, y - iy, z - iz
    ux, uy, uz = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx), fy * fy * (3 - 2 * fy), fz * fz * (3 - 2 * fz)
    r = 0.0
    for dx in (0, 1):
        for dy in (0, 1):
            for dz in (0, 1):
                w = (ux if dx else 1 - ux) * (uy if dy else 1 - uy) * (uz if dz else 1 - uz)
                r = r + w * _hash(ix + dx, iy + dy, iz + dz, seed)
    return r * 2 - 1

def vnoise2(x, y, seed=0):
    return vnoise3(x, y, np.zeros_like(x) + 0.5, seed)

def fbm2(x, y, seed=0, oct=4, lac=2.0, gain=0.5):
    a, f, s = 1.0, 1.0, 0.0
    for o in range(oct):
        s = s + a * vnoise2(x * f, y * f, seed + 17 * o); a *= gain; f *= lac
    return s

def fbm3(x, y, z, seed=0, oct=3):
    a, f, s = 1.0, 1.0, 0.0
    for o in range(oct):
        s = s + a * vnoise3(x * f, y * f, z * f, seed + 31 * o); a *= 0.5; f *= 2.0
    return s

def norm(v):
    return v / (np.linalg.norm(v, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + 1e-12)

def smoothstep(a, b, x):
    t = np.clip((x - a) / (b - a), 0, 1); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t)

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# scene
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Scene:
    def __init__(self, size="medium", light="left", distance="10m", seed=0, tree_density=0.55):
        self.seed = seed; rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.rng = rng
        self.D = DISTS[distance]
        self.size = size; self.light = light; self.distance = distance
        a = SIZES[size] / 2 * rng.uniform(0.9, 1.1)
        self.a = a
        # weathered granite corestone: a rounded block ("woolsack"), long axis a
        self.half = a * np.array([1.0, rng.uniform(0.62, 0.78), rng.uniform(0.72, 0.9)])
        self.rr = 0.62 * self.half.min()
        self.byaw = rng.uniform(-0.7, 0.7)
        self.sink = rng.uniform(0.25, 0.4)
        self.crack = rng.uniform() < 0.55 and size != "small"
        self.crack_n = norm(np.array([rng.uniform(-1, 1), rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3), rng.uniform(-0.4, 0.4)]))
        self.crack_o = rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35) * a
        self.bx, self.bz = 0.0, self.D
        self.shore_wob = rng.uniform(0, 100)
        self.tarn_w = rng.uniform(70, 110)
        self.zfar = self.D + self.tarn_w
        self.tree_density = tree_density
        # boulder centre y: ground at its footprint minus sink
        g = self.ground_h(np.array([self.bx]), np.array([self.bz]))[0]
        self.by = g + self.half[1] * (1 - 2 * self.sink)
        # camera
        cg = self.ground_h(np.array([0.0]), np.array([0.0]))[0]
        self.eye = np.array([0.0, cg + EYE_H, 0.0])
        self.yaw = -math.atan(0.16 * math.tan(HFOV / 2)) + rng.uniform(-0.03, 0.03)
        az = math.radians(LIGHTS[light]) + rng.uniform(-0.08, 0.08) + self.yaw
        self.sun = norm(np.array([math.sin(az) * math.cos(SUN_EL), math.sin(SUN_EL), math.cos(az) * math.cos(SUN_EL)]))
        # light colours (linear): warm low sun, violet sky fill; bounce from sunlit grass
        self.sunC = np.array([1.00, 0.56, 0.30]) * 3.2
        self.skyC = np.array([0.36, 0.30, 0.52]) * 0.85
        self.grassA = np.array([0.30, 0.26, 0.10])
        self.rockA = np.array([0.46, 0.42, 0.38])
        self.bounceC = self.grassA * self.sunC * 0.28 + self.grassA * self.skyC * 0.5
        sh = np.array([self.sun[0], 0, self.sun[2]]); sh = sh / (np.linalg.norm(sh) + 1e-9)
        self.bdir = norm(np.array([-sh[0] * 0.6, -1.0, -sh[2] * 0.6]))  # bounce ARRIVES from below / far side
        self.waterBody = np.array([0.030, 0.024, 0.034])

    # ---- terrain -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    def shore_x(self, v):
        """x of the shoreline (boulder-relative) as a function of v = z - zb. water where u < shore."""
        a = self.a
        s = np.where(v < 0, -0.15 * a + 0.36 * v, -0.15 * a + 1.6 * v)
        s = s + 0.25 * a * fbm2(v * 0.35 + self.shore_wob, np.zeros_like(v) + 3.3, self.seed + 5, 3)
        return s

    def shore_d(self, x, z):
        """signed horizontal distance to the shore, + on the bank"""
        u = x - self.bx; v = z - self.bz
        s = self.shore_x(v)
        slope = np.where(v < 0, 0.36, 1.6)
        return (u - s) / np.sqrt(1 + slope ** 2)

    def ground_h(self, x, z, detail=True):
        d = self.shore_d(x, z)
        lip = 0.22
        bank = -0.06 + (lip + 0.06) * smoothstep(0.0, 0.35, d) + 0.05 * np.clip(d, 0, 40)
        if detail:
            t = fbm2(x * 0.9, z * 0.9, self.seed + 11, 3)      # tussocks, ~1 m
            t2 = fbm2(x * 3.1, z * 3.1, self.seed + 12, 2)     # clumps
            bank = bank + (0.10 * t + 0.035 * t2) * smoothstep(0.1, 0.8, d)
        under = -0.06 + 0.35 * d
        return np.where(d >= 0, bank, np.maximum(under, -1.5))

    # ---- boulder SDF --------------------------------------------------------------------
    def boulder_sdf(self, p):
        c, s = math.cos(self.byaw), math.sin(self.byaw)
        q = p - np.array([self.bx, self.by, self.bz])
        lx = c * q[..., 0] - s * q[..., 2]; lz = s * q[..., 0] + c * q[..., 2]; ly = q[..., 1]
        L = np.stack([lx, ly, lz], -1)
        b = self.half - self.rr
        qq = np.abs(L) - b
        out = np.linalg.norm(np.maximum(qq, 0), axis=-1) + np.minimum(qq.max(-1), 0) - self.rr
        a = self.a
        disp = 0.075 * a * fbm3(lx * 1.1 / a, ly * 1.1 / a, lz * 1.1 / a, self.seed + 21, 3) \
            + 0.018 * a * fbm3(lx * 5 / a, ly * 5 / a, lz * 5 / a, self.seed + 22, 2)
        out = out + disp
        if self.crack:
            slab = np.abs(L @ self.crack_n - self.crack_o) - 0.018 * a
            out = np.maximum(out, -slab)
        return out

    def scene_sdf(self, p):
        tg = (p[..., 1] - self.ground_h(p[..., 0], p[..., 2])) * 0.55
        return np.minimum(tg, self.boulder_sdf(p))

    def material_at(self, p):
        return np.where(self.boulder_sdf(p) < (p[..., 1] - self.ground_h(p[..., 0], p[..., 2])), 2, 1)  # 2 rock, 1 grass

    def normal(self, p, mat):
        e = 0.004 * max(self.a, 1.0)
        n = np.zeros_like(p)
        # rock: SDF gradient; grass: heightfield gradient
        for i in range(3):
            dp = np.zeros(3); dp[i] = e
            n[..., i] = self.boulder_sdf(p + dp) - self.boulder_sdf(p - dp)
        hx = self.ground_h(p[..., 0] + 0.03, p[..., 2]) - self.ground_h(p[..., 0] - 0.03, p[..., 2])
        hz = self.ground_h(p[..., 0], p[..., 2] + 0.03) - self.ground_h(p[..., 0], p[..., 2] - 0.03)
        ng = np.stack([-hx, np.full_like(hx, 0.06), -hz], -1)
        return norm(np.where((mat == 2)[..., None], n, ng))

    # ---- marching -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    def march(self, o, d, tmax, steps=260):
        """sphere-trace the near scene (bank + boulder) up to tmax (per ray). returns t, hit mask"""
        t = np.full(d.shape[:-1], 0.02); hit = np.zeros(d.shape[:-1], bool); alive = np.ones_like(hit)
        for _ in range(steps):
            idx = np.nonzero(alive)
            if idx[0].size == 0: break
            p = o[idx] + d[idx] * t[idx][..., None] if o.ndim > 1 else o + d[idx] * t[idx][..., None]
            h = self.scene_sdf(p)
            eps = 0.0015 * t[idx] + 0.002
            h_hit = h < eps
            hit[tuple(i[h_hit] for i in idx)] = True
            t[idx] = t[idx] + np.maximum(h, 0.004 + 0.002 * t[idx])
            done = h_hit | (t[idx] > tmax[idx])
            alive[tuple(i[done] for i in idx)] = False
        hit &= t <= tmax + 1e-6
        return t, hit

    def soft_shadow(self, p, n):
        o = p + n * 0.03
        res = np.ones(p.shape[:-1]); t = np.full(p.shape[:-1], 0.05)
        for _ in range(72):
            q = o + self.sun * t[..., None]
            h = self.scene_sdf(q)
            res = np.minimum(res, 10.0 * h / t)
            t = t + np.clip(h, 0.04, 1.5)
            if np.all((t > 45) | (res < 0.02)): break
        return np.clip(res, 0, 1)

    def ao(self, p, n):
        occ = np.zeros(p.shape[:-1]); sc = 1.0
        for i, dd in enumerate((0.04, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.55)):
            dd = dd * max(self.a, 0.8)
            occ += (dd - self.scene_sdf(p + n * dd)) * sc; sc *= 0.7
        return np.clip(1 - 1.4 * occ / max(self.a, 0.8), 0, 1)

    # ---- camera -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def basis(self):
        cy, sy, cp, sp = math.cos(self.yaw), math.sin(self.yaw), math.cos(PITCH), math.sin(PITCH)
        fwd = np.array([sy * cp, sp, cy * cp]); right = np.array([cy, 0, -sy]); up = np.cross(fwd, right)
        return fwd, right, -up if up[1] < 0 else up

    def rays(self):
        fwd, right, up = self.basis()
        th = math.tan(HFOV / 2); tv = th * H / W
        xs = ((np.arange(W) + 0.5) / W * 2 - 1) * th
        ys = (1 - (np.arange(H) + 0.5) / H * 2) * tv
        X, Y = np.meshgrid(xs, ys)
        return norm(fwd + X[..., None] * right + Y[..., None] * up)

    def project(self, pts, is_dir=False):
        fwd, right, up = self.basis()
        v = pts if is_dir else pts - self.eye
        zc = v @ fwd; xc = v @ right; yc = v @ up
        th = math.tan(HFOV / 2); tv = th * H / W
        px = (xc / zc / th + 1) / 2 * W - 0.5
        py = (1 - yc / zc / tv) / 2 * H - 0.5
        return px, py, zc

    # ---- sky ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def sky(self, d):
        """continuous dusk sky (linear rgb). observed: pink-peach at the horizon, violet overhead,
        warm glow toward the sun, a pinker band opposite (belt of Venus)."""
        el = np.arcsin(np.clip(d[..., 1], -1, 1))
        e = np.clip(el, 0, None)
        cosg = np.clip(d @ self.sun, -1, 1)
        zen = np.array([0.30, 0.24, 0.48]); mid = np.array([0.62, 0.40, 0.58]); hor = np.array([0.95, 0.56, 0.48])
        t1 = smoothstep(0.0, 0.42, e)[..., None]
        base = hor * (1 - t1) + mid * t1
        t2 = smoothstep(0.12, 0.9, e)[..., None]
        base = base * (1 - t2) + zen * t2
        glow = np.exp(-(1 - cosg) / 0.10)[..., None] * np.array([1.0, 0.62, 0.30]) * 1.4 * np.exp(-e / 0.25)[..., None]
        halo = np.exp(-(1 - cosg) / 0.6)[..., None] * np.array([0.35, 0.18, 0.06]) * np.exp(-e / 0.4)[..., None]
        belt = np.exp(-((el - 0.10) / 0.07) ** 2)[..., None] * smoothstep(0.2, -0.6, cosg)[..., None] * np.array([0.10, 0.02, 0.06])
        disk = (cosg > math.cos(math.radians(0.9)))[..., None] * np.array([3.0, 2.3, 1.2])
        c = (base + glow + halo + belt) * 0.62 + disk
        return c

    # ---- far layers ---------------------------------------------------------------------
    def mountain_ridge(self, az):
        """ridge elevation (rad) per azimuth: distant granite range, ~3 km"""
        r = 0.045 + 0.055 * (0.5 + 0.5 * fbm2(az * 3.0 + self.seed * 0.37, np.zeros_like(az) + 1.7, self.seed + 40, 5))
        r = r + 0.03 * np.maximum(0, fbm2(az * 1.3 + 9.1, np.zeros_like(az), self.seed + 41, 3))
        return r

    def far_layer(self, d):
        """sky + mountains, as a function of direction. returns linear rgb and mask (1 mountain)"""
        az = np.arctan2(d[..., 0], d[..., 2]); el = np.arcsin(np.clip(d[..., 1], -1, 1))
        ridge = self.mountain_ridge(az)
        mnt = el < ridge
        sky = self.sky(d)
        # faceted granite range: ridged noise in angle space -> pseudo normal
        k = 1 / 0.012
        def R(a, e):
            f = 1 - np.abs(fbm2(a * 9 + 3.1, e * 14 + 1.3, self.seed + 43, 4))
            return f * 0.6 + (ridge - e) * 3
        ea, ee = 0.0015, 0.0015
        gx = (R(az + ea, el) - R(az - ea, el)) / (2 * ea) / k
        gy = (R(az, el + ee) - R(az, el - ee)) / (2 * ee) / k
        fwd, right, up = self.basis()
        # face normal: facing camera (-view dir), tilted by gradients
        vd = norm(np.stack([d[..., 0], np.zeros_like(el), d[..., 2]], -1))
        side = np.stack([vd[..., 2], np.zeros_like(el), -vd[..., 0]], -1)
        n = norm(-vd + side * (-gx)[..., None] * 1.3 + np.array([0, 1, 0]) * (0.45 - 0.4 * gy)[..., None])
        ndl = np.clip(n @ self.sun, 0, 1) ** 0.7
        snow = (fbm2(az * 22, el * 30, self.seed + 44, 3) + 0.9 * (el - ridge + 0.04) / 0.04 > 0.55) & (n[..., 1] > 0.35)
        alb = np.where(snow[..., None], np.array([0.80, 0.78, 0.86]), np.array([0.34, 0.30, 0.32]))
        col = alb * (self.sunC * 0.55 * ndl[..., None] + self.skyC * 1.3 * (0.5 + 0.5 * n[..., 1:2]))
        # aerial perspective: 3 km of dusk air
        hz = self.sky(norm(np.stack([d[..., 0], np.full_like(el, 0.02), d[..., 2]], -1)))
        col = col * 0.5 + hz * 0.5
        return np.where(mnt[..., None], col, sky), mnt

    def far_waterline_y(self):
        xs = np.linspace(-2000, 2000, 801)
        px, py, zc = self.project(np.stack([xs, np.zeros_like(xs), np.full_like(xs, self.zfar)], -1))
        return px, py

    def mid_layer(self):
        """far shore bank + conifers, painted as marks on an extended canvas (rows -140..H).
        returns colour canvas (linear rgb), alpha, and the offset."""
        OFF = 140
        HH = H + OFF
        col = np.zeros((HH, W, 3)); al = np.zeros((HH, W))
        mark = np.zeros((HH, W), np.int8)  # 1 bank, 2 tree dark, 3 tree lit, 4 tree mid
        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + 99)
        # far bank: ground rising from waterline over 35 m by ~3 m
        zf = self.zfar
        xs = (np.arange(W) + 0.5)
        th = math.tan(HFOV / 2)
        fwd, right, up = self.basis()
        # per column: waterline row and bank-top row
        rays = self.rays()[0]
        # world x at far plane per column (approx)
        colx = []
        for zz, hh in ((zf, 0.0), (zf + 35, 3.0)):
            pts = []
            for i in range(W):
                d = rays[i]; tt = (zz - self.eye[2]) / d[2]; pts.append(self.eye + d * tt)
            pts = np.array(pts); pts[:, 1] = hh
            colx.append(self.project(pts)[1])
        y_wl, y_top = colx
        bank_top_noise = fbm2(xs * 0.05 + self.seed, np.zeros_like(xs), self.seed + 60, 3) * 1.5
        for x in range(W):
            y0 = int(round(y_wl[x])) + OFF; y1 = int(round(y_top[x] + bank_top_noise[x])) + OFF
            y0 = min(max(y0, 0), HH - 1); y1 = min(max(y1, 0), y0)
            mark[y1:y0 + 1, x] = 1
        # conifers: rooted along the far bank, heights in metres -> pixels
        dist = zf + 12 - self.eye[2]
        pix_ang = HFOV / W
        n_trees = int(W / 6 * self.tree_density)
        xs_t = rng.uniform(-30, W + 30, n_trees)
        # clump them: keep trees where a low-freq noise is high
        keep = fbm2(xs_t * 0.02 + self.seed * 0.1, np.zeros_like(xs_t), self.seed + 61, 2) > -0.15
        xs_t = np.sort(xs_t[keep])
        sunx = self.sun @ right  # + sun to the right on screen
        back = (self.sun @ fwd) > 0.3
        for x0 in xs_t:
            hm = rng.uniform(7, 19) * (1 if rng.uniform() > 0.2 else 0.5)
            hp = hm / dist / pix_ang
            xi = int(round(x0)); base = int(round(np.interp(x0, np.arange(W), y_top))) + OFF + rng.integers(0, 3)
            wmax = max(1.5, hp * rng.uniform(0.22, 0.3))
            tier = max(2, int(round(hp / rng.uniform(5, 8))))
            for r in range(int(hp)):
                y = base - int(hp) + r
                if y < 0 or y >= HH: continue
                f = r / hp
                ph = (r % tier) / tier
                hw = wmax * f * (0.55 + 0.45 * ph) + (0.5 if r > 1 else 0)
                jitter = rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6)
                xa = int(round(x0 - hw + jitter)); xb = int(round(x0 + hw + jitter))
                for x in range(max(xa, 0), min(xb, W - 1) + 1):
                    m = 2
                    edge_sun = (x == xb and sunx > 0.2) or (x == xa and sunx < -0.2)
                    if edge_sun and ph > 0.25: m = 3
                    elif rng.uniform() < 0.18: m = 4
                    if back and (x == xa or x == xb) and ph > 0.5 and rng.uniform() < 0.5: m = 3
                    mark[y, x] = m
            # trunk visible below lowest tier sometimes
        # colours for marks (linear), light model applied to canonical states
        bankC = self.grassA * (self.sunC * 0.30 * max(0.0, -self.sun @ np.array([0, 0, 1.0]) * 0.5 + 0.35) + self.skyC * 0.9)
        treeA = np.array([0.10, 0.12, 0.05])
        treeD = treeA * self.skyC * 0.6
        treeM = treeA * (self.skyC * 1.0 + self.sunC * 0.05)
        treeL = self.grassA * (self.sunC * 0.35 + self.skyC)
        haze = self.sky(norm(np.array([0.0, 0.02, 1.0])))
        amt = min(0.55, (zf / 260.0))
        cols = {1: bankC, 2: treeD, 3: treeL, 4: treeM}
        for m, c in cols.items():
            cc = c * (1 - amt * 0.6) + haze * amt * 0.6 * (0.35 if m != 1 else 0.5)
            col[mark == m] = cc; al[mark == m] = 1
        # stipple on bank: tussock light/dark flecks
        bankmask = mark == 1
        nz = rng.uniform(size=bankmask.shape)
        col[bankmask & (nz < 0.25)] *= 0.62
        col[bankmask & (nz > 0.85)] *= 1.3
        return col, al, mark, OFF

    # ---- shading of near-scene hits -----------------------------------------------------
    def shade(self, p, mat, n, vdir):
        vis = self.soft_shadow(p, n)
        ao = self.ao(p, n)
        ndl = np.clip(n @ self.sun, 0, 1)
        rock = mat == 2
        # rough granite: flatter lit plateau and sharper drop at the terminator than Lambert
        sun_rock = ndl ** 0.55
        # grass: a volume of near-vertical blades: some light even on flat ground at 5 deg sun,
        # plus strong light on tussock slopes that face the sun
        sun_grass = 0.22 + 1.05 * ndl
        sunk = np.where(rock, sun_rock, sun_grass) * vis
        skyk = (0.55 + 0.45 * n[..., 1]) * ao
        bnc = np.clip(n @ self.bdir * -1.0, 0, 1)  # bdir points down; faces looking down/away catch it
        bnc = np.clip(n @ (-self.bdir), 0, 1) * ao
        alb = np.where(rock[..., None], self.rockA, self.grassA)
        # albedo variation: drier/greener patches in grass; lichen-mottle on rock
        var = fbm2(p[..., 0] * 0.35, p[..., 2] * 0.35, self.seed + 70, 3)
        alb = alb * (1 + 0.18 * var[..., None] * np.where(rock[..., None], 0.4, 1.0))
        dry = np.clip(0.5 + 0.8 * fbm2(p[..., 0] * 0.12 + 5, p[..., 2] * 0.12, self.seed + 71, 2), 0, 1)
        grass_tint = np.array([1.12, 1.0, 0.72]) * dry[..., None] + np.array([0.85, 1.02, 1.1]) * (1 - dry[..., None])
        alb = np.where(rock[..., None], alb, alb * grass_tint)
        # back-light translucency of grass blades
        trans = np.where(rock, 0, np.clip(-(vdir @ self.sun), 0, 1) ** 3 * vis * 0.9)
        light = self.sunC * (sunk + trans)[..., None] + self.skyC * skyk[..., None] + self.bounceC * bnc[..., None] * np.where(rock, 1.0, 0.3)[..., None]
        col = alb * light
        return col, dict(vis=vis, ao=ao, ndl=ndl, trans=trans)

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# stage 1: observation render
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SKY, WATER, GRASS, ROCK, FAR = 0, 3, 1, 2, 4

def render_observation(sc):
    d = sc.rays()
    o = sc.eye
    # water plane and far plane bounds
    tw = np.where(d[..., 1] < -1e-6, (0 - o[1]) / np.minimum(d[..., 1], -1e-6), np.inf)
    tfar = np.where(d[..., 2] > 1e-6, (sc.zfar - o[2]) / np.maximum(d[..., 2], 1e-6), np.inf)
    tmax = np.minimum(np.minimum(tw, tfar), 400.0)
    t, hit = sc.march(np.broadcast_to(o, d.shape).copy(), d, tmax)
    p = o + d * t[..., None]
    mat = np.full((H, W), SKY, np.int8)
    water = (~hit) & (tw < tfar)
    mat[water] = WATER
    matn = sc.material_at(p)
    mat[hit] = matn[hit]
    depth = np.where(hit, t, np.where(water, tw, np.inf))
    col = np.zeros((H, W, 3))
    info = {k: np.zeros((H, W)) for k in ("vis", "ao", "ndl", "trans")}
    nrm = np.zeros((H, W, 3))
    if hit.any():
        n = sc.normal(p[hit], mat[hit])
        c, inf = sc.shade(p[hit], mat[hit], n, d[hit])
        col[hit] = c; nrm[hit] = n
        for k in info: info[k][hit] = inf[k]
    # far: mid layer (bank, trees) over far layer (sky, mountains)
    midc, mida, midm, OFF = sc.mid_layer()
    farc, mnt = sc.far_layer(d)
    far = (~hit) & (~water)
    col[far] = farc[far]
    mat[far & mnt] = FAR
    mid_here = (mida[OFF:OFF + H] > 0) & far
    col[mid_here] = midc[OFF:OFF + H][mid_here]
    mat[mid_here] = FAR + 1   # 5 = far shore bank/trees
    # water: Fresnel mirror of everything, with horizontally-correlated ripple normals
    refl_mat = np.full((H, W), -1, np.int8)
    if water.any():
        pw = o + d[water] * tw[water][..., None]
        rng = np.random.default_rng(sc.seed + 5)
        yy, xx = np.nonzero(water)
        # ripple: elongated noise (long in x, short in y) -> horizontal streaks
        rip = fbm2(xx * 0.06 + sc.seed, yy * 0.9, sc.seed + 80, 3)
        amp = math.radians(0.35)
        nrm_w = norm(np.stack([np.zeros_like(rip), np.ones_like(rip), rip * amp * 2.0], -1))
        dv = d[water]
        dr = dv - 2 * (dv @ np.array([0, 1.0, 0]))[..., None] * nrm_w * 0 - 2 * np.sum(dv * nrm_w, -1)[..., None] * nrm_w
        dr = norm(dr)
        # wind lines: sparse horizontal segments where the surface tilts -> sees higher sky
        wl = np.zeros(len(yy))
        segs = rng.integers(0, 1, 1)
        wmask = np.zeros((H, W))
        nseg = int(0.04 * water.sum() / 20) + 3
        for _ in range(nseg):
            ys = rng.integers(0, H); xs0 = rng.integers(0, W); L = int(rng.integers(6, 50) * (1.0 - ys / H * 0.6))
            wmask[ys, xs0:xs0 + L] = 1
        wl = wmask[yy, xx]
        dr = norm(dr + np.stack([np.zeros_like(wl), wl * math.radians(2.5), np.zeros_like(wl)], -1))
        tmx = np.full(len(yy), 200.0)
        tfar_r = np.where(dr[:, 2] > 1e-6, (sc.zfar - pw[:, 2]) / np.maximum(dr[:, 2], 1e-6), np.inf)
        tmx = np.minimum(tmx, tfar_r)
        tr, hr = sc.march(pw + dr * 0.01, dr, tmx, steps=200)
        rc = np.zeros((len(yy), 3))
        rmat = np.full(len(yy), -1, np.int8)
        if hr.any():
            ph = pw[hr] + dr[hr] * tr[hr][..., None]
            mh = sc.material_at(ph)
            nh = sc.normal(ph, mh)
            ch, _ = sc.shade(ph, mh, nh, dr[hr])
            rc[hr] = ch; rmat[hr] = mh
        miss = ~hr
        if miss.any():
            # mid layer via its point on the far plane; else far layer by direction
            q = pw[miss] + dr[miss] * np.minimum(tfar_r[miss], 1e5)[..., None]
            qpx, qpy, qz = sc.project(q)
            qx = np.clip(np.round(qpx).astype(int), 0, W - 1); qy = np.round(qpy).astype(int) + OFF
            okm = (qy >= 0) & (qy < H + OFF) & np.isfinite(tfar_r[miss]) & (qz > 0)
            qy = np.clip(qy, 0, H + OFF - 1)
            midhit = okm & (mida[qy, qx] > 0)
            fc, fm = sc.far_layer(dr[miss])
            cm = np.where(midhit[..., None], midc[qy, qx], fc)
            rc[miss] = cm
            rmat[miss] = np.where(midhit, 5, np.where(fm, FAR, SKY))
        # Fresnel (Schlick, n=1.33) on the rippled normal
        cos_i = np.clip(-np.sum(dv * nrm_w, -1), 0, 1)
        R = 0.02 + 0.98 * (1 - cos_i) ** 5
        col[water] = rc * R[..., None] + sc.waterBody * (1 - R[..., None]) * (sc.skyC / sc.skyC.mean())
        refl_mat[water] = rmat
    return dict(col=col, mat=mat, depth=depth, nrm=nrm, refl_mat=refl_mat, midmark=midm[OFF:OFF + H], **info)

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# tone + colour helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
EXPOSURE = 1.0

def tone(c):
    c = np.maximum(c, 0) * EXPOSURE
    c = c / (1 + 0.35 * c)                 # gentle shoulder
    return np.where(c <= 0.0031308, 12.92 * c, 1.055 * np.power(np.clip(c, 0, None), 1 / 2.4) - 0.055).clip(0, 1)

def to_lab(srgb):
    return rgb2lab(np.asarray(srgb, float).reshape(-1, 1, 3)).reshape(np.shape(srgb))

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# stage 2a: palette from canonical light states
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def kmeans_ramp(samples_srgb, k, key, seed=0):
    from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
    lab = to_lab(samples_srgb)
    if len(lab) < k: k = max(1, len(lab))
    km = KMeans(k, n_init=3, random_state=seed).fit(lab)
    cen = km.cluster_centers_
    order = np.argsort(key(cen, km, lab))
    return lab2rgb(cen[order].reshape(-1, 1, 3)).reshape(-1, 3).clip(0, 1)

def build_palette(sc, obs):
    """32 colours, allotted by material:  sky 8, grass 9, rock 7, far 4, water 3, sun/accent 1.
    grass and rock ramps are the light model evaluated at canonical light states (what the photos
    showed: contact / core / bounce-lit shadow / half / lit / rim for rock; gap / shadow / lit /
    lit-tip for grass). sky, far and water ramps are k-means of the observed continuous colours."""
    pal = {}
    s = sc
    # ---- grass: states (sun, sky, bounce, ao)
    gA = s.grassA
    dry = gA * np.array([1.12, 1.0, 0.72])
    states_g = [(0.0, 0.18, 0.05, gA * 0.8), (0.0, 0.45, 0.2, gA), (0.0, 0.8, 0.4, gA), (0.10, 1.0, 0.5, gA),
                (0.25, 1.0, 0.5, dry), (0.45, 1.0, 0.5, dry), (0.75, 1.0, 0.5, dry), (1.05, 1.0, 0.5, dry), (1.5, 1.0, 0.5, dry * 1.1)]
    pal["grass"] = np.array([tone(a * (s.sunC * su + s.skyC * sk + s.bounceC * 0.3 * b)) for su, sk, b, a in states_g])
    # ---- rock
    rA = s.rockA
    states_r = [(0.0, 0.10, 0.05), (0.0, 0.30, 0.15), (0.0, 0.62, 0.35), (0.0, 0.85, 0.8), (0.30, 0.85, 0.3), (0.62, 0.85, 0.1), (1.0, 0.9, 0.0)]
    pal["rock"] = np.array([tone(rA * (s.sunC * su + s.skyC * sk + s.bounceC * b)) for su, sk, b in states_r])
    # ---- sky (visible sky + what water reflects)
    col = obs["col"]; mat = obs["mat"]
    sky_px = tone(col[mat == SKY]) if (mat == SKY).any() else np.zeros((1, 3))
    # sample the sky up to 40 deg elevation too (reflected in near water)
    fwd, right, up = s.basis()
    az = np.linspace(-0.6, 0.6, 30) + s.yaw; el = np.linspace(0.0, 0.7, 40)
    A, E = np.meshgrid(az, el)
    dd = np.stack([np.sin(A) * np.cos(E), np.sin(E), np.cos(A) * np.cos(E)], -1).reshape(-1, 3)
    sk = s.sky(dd); sk = sk[(dd @ s.sun) < math.cos(math.radians(1.5))]
    samp = np.concatenate([sky_px, tone(sk)])
    pal["sky"] = kmeans_ramp(samp, 8, lambda c, km, lab: c[:, 0], s.seed)
    far_px = col[(mat == FAR)]
    pal["far"] = kmeans_ramp(tone(far_px), 4, lambda c, km, lab: c[:, 0], s.seed) if len(far_px) > 8 else \
        kmeans_ramp(tone(s.far_layer(dd[:200])[0]), 4, lambda c, km, lab: c[:, 0], s.seed)
    wat = col[mat == WATER]
    pal["water"] = kmeans_ramp(tone(wat), 3, lambda c, km, lab: c[:, 0], s.seed) if len(wat) > 8 else np.zeros((3, 3))
    back_sun = (s.sun @ s.basis()[0]) > 0.3
    pal["accent"] = np.array([tone(s.sunC * 0.9)]) if back_sun else np.array([tone(rA * (s.sunC * 1.3 + s.skyC))])
    return pal

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# stage 2b: painting
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
BAYER2 = np.array([[0, 2], [3, 1]]) / 4.0

class Painter:
    def __init__(self, sc, obs, pal):
        self.sc, self.obs, self.pal = sc, obs, pal
        self.names = ["sky", "grass", "rock", "far", "water", "accent"]
        cols = []; self.ramp_idx = {}
        for n in self.names:
            i0 = len(cols); cols += list(pal[n]); self.ramp_idx[n] = np.arange(i0, len(cols))
        self.P = np.clip(np.array(cols), 0, 1)
        assert len(self.P) <= 32, len(self.P)
        self.Plab = to_lab(self.P)
        self.img = np.zeros((H, W), np.int16)
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(sc.seed + 1234)
        yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]; self.yy, self.xx = yy, xx
        self.checker = ((xx + yy) & 1).astype(float)

    def ramp_pos(self, lab, ramp):
        """position along a ramp polyline (in Lab) -> float index"""
        R = self.Plab[self.ramp_idx[ramp]]
        best = np.full(lab.shape[:-1], np.inf); pos = np.zeros(lab.shape[:-1])
        if len(R) == 1: return pos
        for i in range(len(R) - 1):
            a, b = R[i], R[i + 1]; ab = b - a
            t = np.clip(((lab - a) @ ab) / (ab @ ab + 1e-9), 0, 1)
            dist = np.linalg.norm(lab - (a + t[..., None] * ab), axis=-1)
            m = dist < best; best[m] = dist[m]; pos[m] = i + t[m]
        return pos

    def pick(self, ramp, pos, thresh):
        """dither between ramp[floor(pos)] and ramp[floor(pos)+1] using threshold field"""
        idx = self.ramp_idx[ramp]
        i0 = np.clip(np.floor(pos).astype(int), 0, len(idx) - 1)
        f = pos - np.floor(pos)
        i1 = np.clip(i0 + 1, 0, len(idx) - 1)
        return np.where(f > thresh, idx[i1], idx[i0])

    def checker_thresh(self, f_shape_mask=None):
        # 3-level: <1/3 -> A, >2/3 -> B, between -> 50% checker   (Ferrari's sky/water)
        return np.where(self.checker > 0, 0.36, 0.64)

    # ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    def paint(self):
        o = self.obs; sc = self.sc
        srgb = tone(o["col"]); lab = to_lab(srgb)
        mat = o["mat"]
        img = self.img
        # --- sky: bands + checker strips
        m = mat == SKY
        pos = self.ramp_pos(lab, "sky")
        img[m] = self.pick("sky", pos, self.checker_thresh())[m]
        # sun disk/glitter accent
        sunny = m & (srgb.sum(-1) > 2.75)
        if (sc.sun @ sc.basis()[0]) > 0.3: img[sunny] = self.ramp_idx["accent"][0]
        # --- far mountains: facets, checker between, speckle
        m = mat == FAR
        pos = self.ramp_pos(lab, "far")
        th = np.where(self.rng.uniform(size=(H, W)) < 0.5, self.checker_thresh(), self.rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8, (H, W)))
        img[m] = self.pick("far", pos, th)[m]
        # --- far shore bank + trees (mid layer): marks already placed; map colours to grass/far ramps
        m = mat == FAR + 1
        if m.any():
            posg = self.ramp_pos(lab, "grass")
            img[m] = self.pick("grass", posg, self.rng.uniform(0, 1, (H, W)))[m]
        # --- water
        self.paint_water(lab, srgb)
        # --- rock
        self.paint_rock(lab, srgb)
        # --- grass (strokes, far to near, z-tested; overlap water/rock)
        self.paint_grass(lab, srgb)
        return img

    def paint_water(self, lab, srgb):
        o = self.obs; m = o["mat"] == WATER
        if not m.any(): return
        # candidates: all palette colours except accent unless glitter; nearest two, dithered with
        # horizontal-run thresholds (reflections read as horizontal dashes)
        cand = np.concatenate([self.ramp_idx[n] for n in ("sky", "water", "far", "grass", "rock")])
        yy, xx = np.nonzero(m)
        L = lab[m]
        d = np.linalg.norm(L[:, None, :] - self.Plab[cand][None], axis=-1)
        o2 = np.argsort(d, 1)[:, :2]
        a = cand[o2[:, 0]]; b = cand[o2[:, 1]]
        da = d[np.arange(len(L)), o2[:, 0]]; db = d[np.arange(len(L)), o2[:, 1]]
        f = da / (da + db + 1e-9)       # 0 -> a, 0.5 -> between
        # horizontal-run threshold: constant along random runs of 2..6 px
        run = np.zeros((H, W))
        for y in range(H):
            x = 0
            while x < W:
                L_ = self.rng.integers(2, 7); run[y, x:x + L_] = self.rng.uniform(); x += L_
        refl_sky = np.isin(o["refl_mat"][m], (SKY,))
        th = np.where(refl_sky, np.where(self.checker[m] > 0, 0.30, 0.70), 0.15 + 0.7 * run[m])
        # f in [0,.5]: choose b when f > th*0.5
        pick = np.where(f > th * 0.5, b, a)
        self.img[yy, xx] = pick
        # glitter under a back-lit sun
        if (self.sc.sun @ self.sc.basis()[0]) > 0.3:
            g = (srgb[m].sum(-1) > 2.3)
            self.img[yy[g], xx[g]] = self.ramp_idx["accent"][0]
        # far-shore light line: the first water row under the far bank
        mat = o["mat"]
        for x in range(W):
            col = np.nonzero(m[:, x])[0]
            if len(col) == 0: continue
            y0 = col[0]
            if y0 > 0 and mat[y0 - 1, x] in (FAR + 1, FAR, SKY) and self.rng.uniform() < 0.8:
                light = self.ramp_idx["sky"][-3] if len(self.ramp_idx["sky"]) >= 3 else self.ramp_idx["sky"][-1]
                self.img[y0, x] = light

    def paint_rock(self, lab, srgb):
        o = self.obs; m = o["mat"] == ROCK
        if not m.any(): return
        pos = self.ramp_pos(lab, "rock")
        # granite speckle: small-scale noise nudges the ramp position (reads as texture up close)
        n_pix = m.sum()
        spk = self.rng.normal(0, 0.28, (H, W)) * (1.0 if n_pix > 400 else 0.4)
        # lit/shadow family split kept crisp: dither only near the terminator & turning edges
        th = np.where(self.checker > 0, 0.4, 0.6)
        idx = self.pick("rock", np.clip(pos + spk * (pos > 0.8), 0, None), th)
        r = self.ramp_idx["rock"]
        # contact line: occlusion where rock meets ground -> darkest
        ao = o["ao"]
        idx = np.where(m & (ao < 0.45), r[0], idx)
        # rim: silhouette pixels facing the sun get the top value
        nrm = o["nrm"]; ndl = o["ndl"]; vis = o["vis"]
        sun_scr = np.array([self.sc.sun @ self.sc.basis()[1], self.sc.sun @ self.sc.basis()[2]])
        edge = np.zeros_like(m)
        dx = int(np.sign(sun_scr[0])) if abs(sun_scr[0]) > 0.25 else 0
        for ddx, ddy in ((dx, 0), (0, -1)):
            if ddx == 0 and ddy == 0: continue
            sh = np.roll(np.roll(m, -ddy, 0), -ddx, 1)
            edge |= m & ~sh
        rim = edge & (ndl > 0.25) & (vis > 0.5)
        idx = np.where(rim, r[-1], idx)
        # back-light: rim on the top silhouette edge
        if (self.sc.sun @ self.sc.basis()[0]) > 0.3:
            top = m & ~np.roll(m, 1, 0)
            idx = np.where(top & (nrm[..., 1] > 0.2), r[-2], idx)
        self.img[m] = idx[m]

    def paint_grass(self, lab, srgb):
        o = self.obs; sc = self.sc; mat = o["mat"]
        m = mat == GRASS
        if not m.any(): return
        pos = self.ramp_pos(lab, "grass")
        r = self.ramp_idx["grass"]; nr = len(r)
        depth = o["depth"]
        rng = self.rng
        # base: stipple dither (isotropic) - this is what survives in the distance
        base = self.pick("grass", pos, rng.uniform(0, 1, (H, W)))
        self.img[m] = base[m]
        # blade length in px: real blade ~0.3 m, projected; capped
        pix_ang = HFOV / W
        blade_m = 0.28
        Lpx = blade_m / np.maximum(depth, 0.1) / pix_ang * 0.55
        # taller grass/sedge ring at the boulder foot and along the water's lip
        p_world = sc.eye + sc.rays() * np.where(np.isfinite(depth), depth, 0)[..., None]
        bd = sc.boulder_sdf(p_world)
        foot = np.clip(1 - bd / (0.45 * sc.a + 0.15), 0, 1)
        sd = sc.shore_d(p_world[..., 0], p_world[..., 2])
        lipz = np.clip(1 - sd / 0.6, 0, 1)
        Lpx = Lpx * (1 + 1.1 * foot + 0.8 * lipz)
        Lpx = np.clip(Lpx, 0, 14)
        wind = rng.uniform(-0.25, 0.1)
        dep = depth.copy()
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(m & (Lpx >= 1.6))
        order = np.argsort(dep[ys, xs])[::-1]   # far first
        ys, xs = ys[order], xs[order]
        img = self.img
        vis = o["vis"]; trans = o["trans"]
        for y, x in zip(ys, xs):
            L = Lpx[y, x]
            if rng.uniform() > min(1.0, 1.6 / L + 0.15): continue
            p0 = pos[y, x] + rng.normal(0, 0.35)
            gap = rng.uniform() < 0.38
            ln = L * rng.uniform(0.55, 1.25) * (0.7 if gap else 1.0)
            lean = wind + rng.normal(0, 0.18)
            curve = rng.normal(0, 0.02)
            zroot = dep[y, x]
            n = max(1, int(round(ln)))
            lit_tip = (vis[y, x] > 0.5) or (trans[y, x] > 0.2)
            fx = float(x)
            for k in range(n):
                yy = y - k
                if yy < 0: break
                fx += lean + curve * k
                xx = int(round(fx))
                if xx < 0 or xx >= W: break
                # z-test: only paint over things farther than the root
                if dep[yy, xx] < zroot - 0.05 and mat[yy, xx] != GRASS: break
                if mat[yy, xx] == GRASS and dep[yy, xx] < zroot - 0.3: break
                f = k / max(n - 1, 1)
                if gap:
                    pi = p0 - 2.2 + 0.6 * f
                else:
                    pi = p0 - 1.1 + 1.6 * f + (0.9 * f if lit_tip else 0)
                img[yy, xx] = r[int(np.clip(round(pi), 0, nr - 1))]

# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def paint_scene(size="medium", light="left", distance="10m", seed=0, return_obs=False):
    sc = Scene(size, light, distance, seed)
    obs = render_observation(sc)
    pal = build_palette(sc, obs)
    pt = Painter(sc, obs, pal)
    idx = pt.paint()
    rgb = (pt.P[idx] * 255).round().astype(np.uint8)
    if return_obs:
        return rgb, (tone(obs["col"]) * 255).astype(np.uint8), pt.P, obs
    return rgb

def up3(rgb):
    return np.repeat(np.repeat(rgb, 3, 0), 3, 1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--size", default="medium"); ap.add_argument("--light", default="left")
    ap.add_argument("--distance", default="10m"); ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="out/single.png")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    rgb, obsimg, P, obs = paint_scene(a.size, a.light, a.distance, a.seed, True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(a.out) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    Image.fromarray(up3(rgb)).save(a.out)
    Image.fromarray(up3(obsimg)).save(a.out.replace(".png", "_obs.png"))
    print("colours used:", len(np.unique(rgb.reshape(-1, 3), axis=0)), "palette:", len(P))

Bash: Measure Ferrari's contour run and step structure
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && cat > ferrari_contours.py <<'EOF'
"""How Ferrari's contours resolve into pixel runs.
For a silhouette edge y_top(x) (first 'object' pixel per column), record the run structure:
horizontal run lengths at constant y, and the size of each vertical step between runs.
Clean steps: run 1 & step 1 = 45 deg; run 2 & step 1 = 2:1; run 1 & step 2 = 1:2, etc."""
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from collections import Counter
a = np.array(Image.open("ferrari/native.png").convert("RGB")).astype(int)
def is_sky(c):  # the pink/violet sky ramp: bright, R>=G, B high-ish
    r, g, b = c[..., 0], c[..., 1], c[..., 2]
    return (r > 170) & (g > 140) & (b > 140)
def contour_runs(y_top):
    runs, steps = [], []
    x = 0
    while x < len(y_top):
        x2 = x
        while x2 + 1 < len(y_top) and y_top[x2 + 1] == y_top[x]: x2 += 1
        runs.append(x2 - x + 1)
        if x2 + 1 < len(y_top): steps.append(abs(int(y_top[x2 + 1]) - int(y_top[x])))
        x = x2 + 1
    return runs, steps
def report(name, y_top):
    runs, steps = contour_runs(y_top)
    pairs = Counter(zip(runs[:-1], steps))
    print(f"== {name}: {len(runs)} runs")
    print("   run length hist :", sorted(Counter(runs).items())[:12])
    print("   step size hist  :", sorted(Counter(steps).items())[:10])
    tot = sum(pairs.values())
    print("   top (run,step)  :", [(k, round(v / tot, 2)) for k, v in pairs.most_common(8)])
    # consistency: fraction of consecutive run pairs equal (a clean slope repeats its run length)
    same = np.mean([abs(runs[i] - runs[i + 1]) <= 0 for i in range(len(runs) - 1)])
    print(f"   consecutive equal runs: {same:.2f}")
# mountain ridge vs sky, x 180..420 (native)
S = is_sky(a)
yt = np.array([np.argmax(~S[40:200, x]) + 40 for x in range(190, 440)])
report("mountain ridge (native x190-440)", yt)
# island top edge: grass pixels are yellow-green (g>b)
def is_grass(c): return (c[..., 1] >= c[..., 2] + 8)
G = is_grass(a)
yt = np.array([np.argmax(G[255:300, x]) + 255 for x in range(275, 410)])
report("island mound top (x275-410)", yt)
# left bank edge against water, per row: first non-grass pixel from left in rows 300..380
xr = np.array([np.argmax(~G[y, 60:330]) + 60 for y in range(285, 330)])
report("left bank lip, per row (y285-330)", xr)
EOF

Write: pedagogy/observation/pix.py
"""
pix.py -- small pixel-craft primitives learned from Ferrari's pixels (see translate_studies.py).

 clean_profile   a contour as runs: flat runs, 45 deg (1:1), 2:1, 1:2 ... held consistently
 majority_clean  remove isolated single pixels in a zone map (his zones are shapes, not noise)
 stipple         random-threshold dither between two colours (organic transitions: grass, rock)
 checker         3-level ordered dither: A | 50% checker | B  (smooth things: sky, water, snow)
"""
import numpy as np

def clean_profile(yf, max_run=14):
    """float y per column -> int y per column made of consistent runs.
    |slope| < 1 : horizontal runs of n = round(1/|slope|) px then a 1 px step
    |slope| >= 1: 1 px runs with vertical steps of k = round(|slope|)
    the pattern is held until the contour drifts more than 1 px from the true curve."""
    yf = np.asarray(yf, float); n = len(yf)
    if n == 0: return yf.astype(int)
    out = np.zeros(n, int); y = int(round(yf[0])); out[0] = y
    run_len, run_pos, step = 1, 0, 0
    def choose(x):
        x2 = min(n - 1, x + 3); x1 = max(0, x - 1)
        s = (yf[x2] - yf[x1]) / max(x2 - x1, 1)
        if abs(s) < 1.0 / max_run: return max_run, 0
        if abs(s) < 1: return int(np.clip(round(1 / abs(s)), 1, max_run)), int(np.sign(s))
        return 1, int(np.sign(s) * round(abs(s)))
    run_len, step = choose(0)
    for x in range(1, n):
        run_pos += 1
        if run_pos >= run_len:
            y += step; run_pos = 0
            run_len, step = choose(x)
        # re-sync when drifting
        if abs(y - yf[x]) > 1.0:
            y = int(round(yf[x])); run_pos = 0; run_len, step = choose(x)
        out[x] = y
    return out

def majority_clean(z, iters=1, protect=None):
    """replace pixels that disagree with both horizontal or both vertical neighbours, when those
    neighbours agree with each other (kills single-pixel noise, keeps 1-px lines that run)."""
    z = z.copy()
    for _ in range(iters):
        l = np.roll(z, 1, 1); r = np.roll(z, -1, 1); u = np.roll(z, 1, 0); d = np.roll(z, -1, 0)
        iso_h = (l == r) & (z != l); iso_v = (u == d) & (z != u)
        iso = iso_h & iso_v
        if protect is not None: iso &= ~protect
        z = np.where(iso, l, z)
    return z

def stipple(t, rng, bias=0.0):
    """True where colour B should be used, given fraction t in [0,1] (white-noise threshold)."""
    return t + bias > rng.uniform(0, 1, np.shape(t))

def checker(t, yy, xx):
    """3-level: t<1/3 -> A (False), t>2/3 -> B (True), otherwise 50% checkerboard"""
    c = ((xx + yy) & 1) == 1
    return np.where(t < 1 / 3, False, np.where(t > 2 / 3, True, c))

def hex2rgb(h):
    h = h.lstrip("#"); return np.array([int(h[i:i + 2], 16) for i in (0, 2, 4)], np.uint8)
Write: pedagogy/observation/translate_studies.py
"""
translate_studies.py -- one observed truth at a time, translated into pixels, shown beside the
photo it came from and beside Ferrari's pixels for the nearest thing in his tarn, all at 8x.

  python translate_studies.py D      -> studies/translate_D.png  etc.
"""
import sys, numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
from pix import clean_profile, majority_clean, stipple, checker, hex2rgb

FER = np.array(Image.open("ferrari/native.png").convert("RGB"))
S = 8

# dusk palette pieces used by the studies (grass from Ferrari's own grass; granite hue-shifted:
# lit toward peach/orange like his lit rock, shadows toward violet like his shadow rock)
C = {k: hex2rgb(v) for k, v in dict(
    rim="F6C8AA", lit="E3A283", half="B48C98", shade="7E6268", core="54435A", contact="241C29",
    g_tan="C4834C", g_ochre="735C23", g_gap="0A220A", g_mid="886532",
    g_dol="3E3B17", g_dk="1C1F04", g_blk="000000",
    w_hi="D9B5CD", w_mid="B690A8", w_lo="8A6E86", w_dk="5A4658",
    sky1="E0A6B0", sky2="CFA2BB").items()}

def photo_crop(path, box, h):
    im = Image.open(path).convert("RGB").crop(box)
    w = int(im.size[0] * h / im.size[1]); return im.resize((w, h), Image.LANCZOS)

def fer_crop(box):
    x0, y0, x1, y1 = box
    return Image.fromarray(FER[y0:y1, x0:x1]).resize(((x1 - x0) * S, (y1 - y0) * S), Image.NEAREST)

def mine(img):
    return Image.fromarray(img).resize((img.shape[1] * S, img.shape[0] * S), Image.NEAREST)

def sheet(name, title, panels):
    h = max(p.size[1] for p, _ in panels)
    w = sum(p.size[0] for p, _ in panels) + 12 * (len(panels) - 1)
    out = Image.new("RGB", (w, h + 40), (245, 245, 240)); d = ImageDraw.Draw(out); x = 0
    for p, cap in panels:
        out.paste(p, (x, 0)); d.text((x + 2, h + 4), cap, fill=(0, 0, 0)); x += p.size[0] + 12
    d.text((2, h + 22), title, fill=(90, 0, 0))
    out.save(f"studies/translate_{name}.png"); print("wrote", name)

def paint(zone, colmap):
    img = np.zeros(zone.shape + (3,), np.uint8)
    for k, c in colmap.items(): img[zone == k] = C[c]
    return img

# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# D: light wrapping a rounded boulder (sun low, from the left)
# observed (r02 profile): lit plateau, SHARP drop at the terminator, a darker core band just past
# it, then a flatter, warmer bounce-lit shadow side; rim of light where the edge faces the sun.
# Ferrari (mountain facets): each value is a flat plane with a clean stepped edge; lit planes
# carry a brighter 1-px lip along the sun-facing ridge; transitions are short checker strips.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def boulder_zones(w, h, cx, cy, a, b, p=2.6, L=(-0.85, 0.12, 0.5), B=(0.55, -0.75, 0.35), seed=1,
                  crack=True):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w].astype(float)
    # silhouette: superellipse; top contour cleaned into runs
    X = (xx + 0.5 - cx) / a; Y = (cy - (yy + 0.5)) / b
    # top contour per column, flattened bottom (sunk in ground)
    xs = (np.arange(w) + 0.5 - cx) / a
    inside_x = np.abs(xs) < 1
    ytop = np.full(w, np.inf)
    ytop[inside_x] = cy - b * (1 - np.abs(xs[inside_x]) ** p) ** (1 / p)
    # break the perfect symmetry: weathered block leans, crest offset
    ytop = ytop + 0.12 * b * np.sin(xs * 2.1 + seed) * inside_x
    yt = np.full(w, 10 ** 6)
    cols = np.nonzero(inside_x)[0]
    yt[cols] = clean_profile(ytop[cols])
    base = cy + 0.35 * b          # the ground cuts it: partly sunk
    inside = (yy >= yt[None, :]) & (yy <= base) & inside_x[None, :]
    # normal of a superellipsoid bulge
    r = np.clip(np.abs(X) ** p + np.abs(Y) ** p, 0, 1)
    gx = np.sign(X) * np.abs(X) ** (p - 1) / a; gy = np.sign(Y) * np.abs(Y) ** (p - 1) / b
    nz = (1 - r) ** (1 / p) * 1.2 + 0.05
    n = np.stack([gx * a, gy * b, nz], -1); n /= np.linalg.norm(n, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    # big weathered planes: quantise the normal a little (granite reads as planes, not a ball)
    n = n + 0.25 * np.stack([np.round(n[..., 0] * 2) / 2, np.round(n[..., 1] * 2) / 2, n[..., 2] * 0], -1)
    n /= np.linalg.norm(n, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    L = np.array(L) / np.linalg.norm(L); B = np.array(B) / np.linalg.norm(B)
    ndl = n @ L; nb = n @ B
    z = np.full((h, w), -1)
    z[inside & (ndl > 0.55)] = 5                                   # lit face
    z[inside & (ndl <= 0.55) & (ndl > 0.22)] = 4                   # half: top plane, sky + grazing sun
    z[inside & (ndl <= 0.22) & (nb < 0.25)] = 2                    # core band past the terminator
    z[inside & (ndl <= 0.22) & (nb >= 0.25)] = 3                   # bounce-lit shadow side
    z[inside & (ndl <= 0.22) & (ndl > 0.10) & (nb >= 0.25)] = 2    # terminator itself is core
    z = majority_clean(z, 2)
    # lit/half boundary: 1-px checker strip (a rounded turn, not an edge)
    tb = inside & (np.abs(ndl - 0.55) < 0.06)
    z[tb & (((xx + yy).astype(int) & 1) == 1)] = 5
    # rim: silhouette pixel whose neighbour toward the sun is outside, and faces the sun
    left_out = ~np.roll(inside, 1, 1); up_out = ~np.roll(inside, 1, 0)
    z[inside & (left_out | up_out) & (ndl > 0.35)] = 6
    # speckle: sparse flecks of the neighbouring value (granite grain), only inside big zones
    fl = inside & (rng.uniform(size=z.shape) < 0.05) & (z >= 3) & (z <= 5)
    z[fl] = z[fl] - 1
    # a joint crack: dark 1-px line; its lower lip catches light on the sun side
    if crack:
        x0 = int(cx + 0.15 * a); pts = []
        for i in range(int(0.9 * b * 2)):
            y = int(yt[x0] + 1 + i); x = x0 + i // 2
            if 0 <= y < h and 0 <= x < w and inside[y, x] and y < base - 1:
                z[y, x] = 2 if z[y, x] >= 3 else 1
                if x - 1 >= 0 and inside[y, x - 1] and ndl[y, x - 1] > 0.2: z[y, x - 1] = 6 if ndl[y, x - 1] > 0.5 else 5
    return z, inside, yt, base, ndl

def study_D():
    w, h = 44, 30
    z, inside, yt, base, ndl = boulder_zones(w, h, 22, 20, 17, 14)
    bg = np.full((h, w), 0); bg[:14] = 10
    img = np.zeros((h, w, 3), np.uint8)
    img[:] = C["g_ochre"]; img[:14] = C["sky1"]
    cm = {6: "rim", 5: "lit", 4: "half", 3: "shade", 2: "core", 1: "contact"}
    for k, c in cm.items(): img[z == k] = C[c]
    sheet("D", "D  light on a rounded boulder: flat planes, sharp terminator, core band, bounce-lit shadow, sun-side rim",
          [(photo_crop("refs/r02_splitrock_boulders_lowsun_b.jpg", (280, 305, 425, 425), 240), "photo r02 (sun from left)"),
           (fer_crop((296, 98, 350, 128)), "Ferrari: lit/shadow rock facets 8x"),
           (mine(img), "mine 8x")])

if __name__ == "__main__":
    for s in sys.argv[1:]: globals()["study_" + s]()

Bash: Replace boulder study with 3D raycast zones

import re
s = open("translate_studies.py").read()
new_fn = r'''
def sdf_rbox(P, half, rr, seed=1, bump=0.06):
    q = np.abs(P) - (half - rr)
    d = np.linalg.norm(np.maximum(q, 0), axis=-1) + np.minimum(q.max(-1), 0) - rr
    # low-frequency weathering: a few broad dents (not noise at pixel scale)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for _ in range(4):
        c = rng.normal(0, 1, 3); c /= np.linalg.norm(c); c *= half
        d = d + bump * half.max() * np.exp(-np.sum((P - c) ** 2, -1) / (0.35 * half.max()) ** 2)
    return d

def raycast_boulder(w, h, half, rr, yaw=0.5, tilt=np.radians(12), scale=1.0, cx=None, base_frac=0.35, seed=1):
    """orthographic view of a rounded granite block, camera tilted down; returns normal map + mask.
    the ground plane cuts the block at base_frac of its height (partly sunk)."""
    cx = w / 2 if cx is None else cx
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w].astype(float)
    # screen -> world ray origins (orthographic)
    ct, st, cy_, sy_ = np.cos(tilt), np.sin(tilt), np.cos(yaw), np.sin(yaw)
    sx = (xx + 0.5 - cx) / scale; syv = (h * 0.62 - (yy + 0.5)) / scale
    fwd = np.array([0, -st, ct]); up = np.array([0, ct, st]); right = np.array([1.0, 0, 0])
    O = sx[..., None] * right + syv[..., None] * up - fwd * 10
    Rm = np.array([[cy_, 0, -sy_], [0, 1, 0], [sy_, 0, cy_]])
    def f(P):
        Pl = P @ Rm.T
        return sdf_rbox(Pl, half, rr, seed)
    t = np.zeros((h, w)); hit = np.zeros((h, w), bool)
    ground = -half[1] + 2 * half[1] * base_frac
    for _ in range(160):
        P = O + fwd * t[..., None]
        d = f(P)
        hit |= d < 1e-3
        t = np.where(hit, t, t + np.maximum(d, 1e-3))
    P = O + fwd * t[..., None]
    hit &= P[..., 1] > ground
    e = 1e-3; n = np.zeros_like(P)
    for i in range(3):
        dp = np.zeros(3); dp[i] = e; n[..., i] = f(P + dp) - f(P - dp)
    n /= np.linalg.norm(n, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + 1e-9
    return n, hit, P, ground

def boulder_zones(w, h, half, rr, L=(-0.9, 0.12, -0.35), B=(0.5, -0.8, -0.2), yaw=0.55, scale=1.0,
                  seed=1, crack=True, cx=None):
    """zones: 6 rim, 5 lit, 4 half, 3 bounce-lit shadow, 2 core, 1 contact"""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n, inside, P, ground = raycast_boulder(w, h, np.array(half), rr, yaw=yaw, scale=scale, seed=seed, cx=cx)
    L = np.array(L) / np.linalg.norm(L); B = np.array(B) / np.linalg.norm(B)
    ndl = n @ L; nb = n @ B
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w]
    z = np.full((h, w), -1)
    z[inside & (ndl > 0.5)] = 5
    z[inside & (ndl <= 0.5) & (ndl > 0.16)] = 4
    z[inside & (ndl <= 0.16) & (nb < 0.3)] = 2
    z[inside & (ndl <= 0.16) & (nb >= 0.3)] = 3
    z[inside & (ndl <= 0.16) & (ndl > 0.02)] = 2      # the terminator band is always core
    z = majority_clean(z, 2)
    # a rounded turn between lit and half: 1-px checker strip; the terminator stays sharp
    tb = inside & (np.abs(ndl - 0.5) < 0.05)
    z[tb & (((xx + yy) & 1) == 1)] = 5
    # rim: silhouette pixels whose sun-side neighbour is outside and which face the sun
    sx = -1 if L[0] < -0.2 else (1 if L[0] > 0.2 else 0)
    out_sun = ~np.roll(inside, -sx, 1) if sx else np.zeros_like(inside)
    out_up = ~np.roll(inside, 1, 0)
    z[inside & (out_sun | out_up) & (ndl > 0.3)] = 6
    # sparse grain flecks, only inside large zones
    fl = inside & (rng.uniform(size=z.shape) < 0.045) & (z >= 3) & (z <= 5)
    z[fl] -= 1
    # contact: the lowest 1 px of the silhouette (occlusion where rock meets ground)
    below_out = ~np.roll(inside, -1, 0)
    z[inside & below_out & (P[..., 1] < ground + 0.12 * half[1] * 2)] = 1
    return z, inside, ndl
'''
s = re.sub(r"def boulder_zones\(.*?\n    return z, inside, yt, base, ndl\n", new_fn.strip("\n") + "\n", s, flags=re.S)
s = s.replace('''    z, inside, yt, base, ndl = boulder_zones(w, h, 22, 20, 17, 14)''', '''    z, inside, ndl = boulder_zones(w, h, (15, 10, 11), 5.5, scale=1.0)''')
s = s.replace('(fer_crop((296, 98, 350, 128)), "Ferrari: lit/shadow rock facets 8x")', '(fer_crop((205, 100, 255, 131)), "Ferrari: rock facets, sun-lit ridges 8x")')
open("translate_studies.py", "w").write(s)

Bash: Add continuous ramp value with clustered dither
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("translate_studies.py").read()
s = s.replace('''def study_D():''', '''def boulder_value(n, P, ground, halfy, L, B, sky_w=0.9):
    """continuous ramp coordinate: 1 contact .. 2 core .. 3 bounce-lit shadow .. 4 half .. 5 lit .. 6 rim.
    The lit side and the shadow side are separate ranges: the terminator is a JUMP (observed r02),
    so dithering never crosses it."""
    ndl = n @ L; nb = n @ B; up = n[..., 1]
    lit = 3.7 + 2.0 * np.clip((ndl - 0.15) / 0.75, 0, 1) ** 0.8
    shd = 2.0 + 1.0 * np.clip(nb / 0.6, 0, 1)
    shd = np.maximum(shd, 2.2 + 1.5 * np.clip((up - 0.3) / 0.6, 0, 1) * sky_w)   # sky-lit tops
    v = np.where(ndl > 0.15, np.maximum(lit, shd), shd)
    # contact occlusion: fades the last ~15% of height toward the contact value
    hgt = (P[..., 1] - ground) / (2 * halfy)
    v = v - np.clip(1 - hgt / 0.16, 0, 1) * (v - 1.0) * 0.85
    return v, ndl

def clustered_noise(h, w, rng):
    """threshold field with small clumps (2-px scale) plus fine grain -- Ferrari's rock mixes
    read as strings and clumps, not salt-and-pepper"""
    c = rng.uniform(size=((h + 1) // 2 + 1, (w + 1) // 2 + 1))
    c = np.repeat(np.repeat(c, 2, 0), 2, 1)[:h, :w]
    off = rng.integers(0, 2, size=h)
    for y in range(h): c[y] = np.roll(c[y], off[y])
    return 0.55 * c + 0.45 * rng.uniform(size=(h, w))

def boulder_paint(w, h, half, rr, L=(-0.9, 0.12, -0.35), B=(0.5, -0.8, -0.2), yaw=0.55, seed=1, cx=None, spread=1.0):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    n, inside, P, ground = raycast_boulder(w, h, np.array(half), rr, yaw=yaw, seed=seed, cx=cx)
    L = np.array(L) / np.linalg.norm(L); B = np.array(B) / np.linalg.norm(B)
    v, ndl = boulder_value(n, P, ground, half[1], L, B)
    th = clustered_noise(h, w, rng)
    lo = np.floor(v); f = v - lo
    # only the fractional part in the middle band dithers; near-integers stay flat (planes!)
    f2 = np.clip((f - 0.5) * spread + 0.5, 0, 1)
    z = np.where(f2 > th, lo + 1, lo).astype(int)
    z = np.clip(z, 1, 6)
    z[~inside] = -1
    # rim: sun-side silhouette pixels that face the sun
    sx = -1 if L[0] < -0.2 else (1 if L[0] > 0.2 else 0)
    out_sun = ~np.roll(inside, -sx, 1) if sx else np.zeros_like(inside)
    out_up = ~np.roll(inside, 1, 0)
    z[inside & (out_sun | out_up) & (ndl > 0.35)] = 6
    # contact: the lowest silhouette pixel row is always the darkest
    below_out = ~np.roll(inside, -1, 0)
    z[inside & below_out] = 1
    return z, inside

def study_D():''')
s = s.replace('''    z, inside, ndl = boulder_zones(w, h, (15, 11, 11), 8.0, scale=1.0)''', '''    z, inside = boulder_paint(w, h, (15, 11, 11), 8.0)''')
open("translate_studies.py", "w").write(s)
EOF

    # surface grain: weathering flutes run down the fall line -> noise stretched vertically.
    g = rng.uniform(-1, 1, (h // 3 + 2, w + 2))

    if crack:
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(inside)
        x0 = int(np.percentile(xs, 55)); ytop = ys[xs == x0].min()
        x = float(x0); dx = rng.uniform(-0.35, 0.35)
        for y in range(ytop, ys.max()):
            xi = int(round(x))
            if not inside[y, xi]: break
            z[y, xi] = min(z[y, xi], 2)
            if xi - 1 >= 0 and inside[y, xi - 1] and z[y, xi - 1] >= 4: z[y, xi - 1] = min(6, z[y, xi - 1] + 1)
            x += dx + rng.normal(0, 0.25)
            if rng.uniform() < 0.12: break
    # contact: the lowest silhouette pixel row is always the darkest
    below_out = ~np.roll(inside, -1, 0)
    z[inside & below_out] = 1
    return z, inside''')

58. pedagogy/observation/ferrari/treebase.png

Bash: Add grass primitive and contact study A
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && cat >> translate_studies.py <<'EOF'

# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# grass primitive (measured from Ferrari: each lighting family uses 3 colours locally --
# lit: ochre base, tan blades, dark-green gaps; shadow: dark olive base, darker olive blades,
# black gaps. blades are 1-px vertical runs of ~3-4 px, leaning by 1-px steps.)
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FAM = {"lit": ("g_ochre", "g_tan", "g_gap"), "shd": ("g_dol", "g_dk", "g_blk")}

def grass_field(img, mask, lit, Lmap, rng, depth=None, occl=None, density=1.0, lean=-0.15, tip_boost=None):
    """paint grass into img where mask. lit: bool map (sunlit family). Lmap: blade length (px).
    blades are painted back-to-front (top rows first) so lower (nearer) blades overlap.
    occl: optional mask of pixels blades may overpaint (e.g. boulder foot, water) - they are
    'behind' the grass."""
    h, w = mask.shape
    for fam, m in (("lit", mask & lit), ("shd", mask & ~lit)):
        base, light, dark = FAM[fam]
        img[m] = C[base]
        t = rng.uniform(size=(h, w))
        img[m & (t < 0.22)] = C[dark]
        img[m & (t > 0.80)] = C[light]
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)
    order = np.argsort(ys)
    can = mask | (occl if occl is not None else False)
    for i in order:
        y, x = ys[i], xs[i]
        L = Lmap[y, x]
        if L < 1.5 or rng.uniform() > density * min(1, 1.8 / L): continue
        fam = "lit" if lit[y, x] else "shd"
        base, light, dark = FAM[fam]
        gap = rng.uniform() < 0.35
        n = int(round(L * rng.uniform(0.6, 1.2)))
        fx = float(x); ln = lean + rng.normal(0, 0.2)
        for k in range(n):
            yy = y - k; xx = int(round(fx))
            if yy < 0 or xx < 0 or xx >= w or not can[yy, xx]: break
            f = k / max(n - 1, 1)
            if gap: c = dark
            else:
                c = light if f > 0.45 else base
                if tip_boost is not None and tip_boost[y, x] and f > 0.7: c = light
            img[yy, xx] = C[c]; fx += ln

# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# A: the contact -- a boulder sitting IN a grass bank, low sun from the left
# observed (r10, r13): darkest line where rock meets ground; an undercut pocket of near-black on
# the shadow side; grass taller at the foot, overlapping the base; long cast shadow on the grass.
# Ferrari (stones at the waterline): the darkest palette colour as a 1-px line under the form;
# shadowed grass switches to a different 3-colour family, it is not the lit grass darkened.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def study_A():
    w, h = 56, 34
    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
    img = np.zeros((h, w, 3), np.uint8)
    horizon = 9
    img[:horizon] = C["sky1"]
    z, inside = boulder_paint(w, h, (11, 9, 9), 6.5, cx=22, seed=4)
    # ground occupies everything below the horizon; the boulder cuts it
    ground = np.zeros((h, w), bool); ground[horizon:] = True
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(inside)
    base_y = np.array([ys[xs == x].max() if (xs == x).any() else -1 for x in range(w)])
    # cast shadow: low sun from left -> long shadow to the right, band hugging the base row
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w]
    bx0, bx1 = xs.min(), xs.max(); by = ys.max()
    shadow = (xx > bx0 + 0.55 * (bx1 - bx0)) & (np.abs(yy - (by - 1 + (xx - bx1) * 0.06)) < 2.4 + (xx - bx1).clip(0) * 0.03)
    shadow |= inside  # beneath the rock
    lit = ground & ~shadow
    # blade length grows toward the viewer (bottom) and at the rock's foot
    Lmap = 1.0 + (yy - horizon).clip(0) / (h - horizon) * 4.5
    foot = np.zeros((h, w))
    for x in range(bx0 - 3, bx1 + 4):
        if 0 <= x < w:
            yb = base_y[min(max(x, bx0), bx1)]
            foot[yb - 1:yb + 3, x] = 1
    Lmap = Lmap + 3.5 * foot
    grass_field(img, ground & ~inside, lit, Lmap, rng, occl=inside & (yy > ys.max() - 5))
    # rock over grass except where blades overlap later: paint rock first, then re-run blades at foot
    cm = {6: "rim", 5: "lit", 4: "half", 3: "shade", 2: "core", 1: "contact"}
    rock = np.zeros_like(img)
    for k, c in cm.items(): rock[z == k] = C[c]
    img[inside] = rock[inside]
    # the undercut pocket: near-black just under the shadow-side base in the grass
    for x in range(int(bx0 + 0.5 * (bx1 - bx0)), bx1 + 1):
        yb = base_y[x]
        if yb + 1 < h: img[yb + 1, x] = C["g_blk"]
    # blades at the foot, in front of the base, overlap the rock's lowest pixels
    footmask = ground & ~inside & (foot > 0)
    grass_field_front(img, footmask, lit, Lmap, rng, occl=inside)
    sheet("A", "A  contact: darkest line under the rock, black undercut pocket, shadow grass = its own family, tufts overlap the foot",
          [(photo_crop("refs/r10_boulder_by_lochan_grassfoot.jpg", (480, 420, 900, 636), 272), "photo r10 (foot, undercut)"),
           (fer_crop((240, 398, 300, 432)), "Ferrari: stones at the waterline 8x"),
           (mine(img), "mine 8x")])

def grass_field_front(img, mask, lit, Lmap, rng, occl):
    """only blades (no base fill), for tufts overlapping something behind them"""
    h, w = mask.shape
    ys, xs = np.nonzero(mask)
    for i in np.argsort(ys):
        y, x = ys[i], xs[i]
        if rng.uniform() > 0.55: continue
        fam = "lit" if lit[y, x] else "shd"
        base, light, dark = FAM[fam]
        n = int(round(Lmap[y, x] * rng.uniform(0.7, 1.2))); fx = float(x); ln = rng.normal(-0.1, 0.25)
        gap = rng.uniform() < 0.3
        for k in range(n):
            yy = y - k; xx = int(round(fx))
            if yy < 0 or xx < 0 or xx >= w: break
            f = k / max(n - 1, 1)
            img[yy, xx] = C[dark if gap else (light if f > 0.45 else base)]; fx += ln
EOF
