Bash: Add grass-mass lit-top study B
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("translate_studies.py").read()
blk = '''if __name__ == "__main__":
    for s in sys.argv[1:]: globals()["study_" + s]()
'''
s = s.replace(blk, "")
s += '''
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# B: the lit top of a grass mass (tussock / bank hump), low sun from the left
# observed (r20, r23): the mass is dark in its body and base; light collects on the crest and on
# the sun-facing shoulder, and the blade tips along the silhouette catch it hardest.
# Ferrari (left bank crest, island): a 1-3 px band of the lightest tan right under the crest
# contour; below it the lit colour is STIPPLED into the shadow family with falling density; the
# contour itself is a long flat run with clean 45 / 2:1 shoulders.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def mound(w, h, y_base, cx, half_w, height, rng, flat=0.45):
    xs = np.arange(w) + 0.5
    u = np.clip(np.abs(xs - cx) / half_w, 0, 1)
    prof = np.where(u < flat, 0, ((u - flat) / (1 - flat)) ** 1.6)   # flat crest, curving shoulders
    yt = y_base - height * (1 - prof)
    cols = np.nonzero(np.abs(xs - cx) < half_w)[0]
    out = np.full(w, 10 ** 6); out[cols] = clean_profile(yt[cols])
    return out

def mound_light(h, w, yt, cx, sun_x=-1, band=2.5, falloff=9.0, rng=None):
    """t in [0,1]: how 'lit' each pixel of the mass is. crest band + sun-facing shoulder."""
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w]
    d = yy - yt[None, :]
    side = np.clip((cx - xx) * (-sun_x) / max(1, np.ptp(np.nonzero(yt < 10 ** 5)[0])) * 2, -1, 1)  # +1 on sun side
    t = np.clip(1 - d / falloff, 0, 1) * (0.55 + 0.45 * side)
    t = np.where(d < band, np.maximum(t, 0.95), t)
    return t, d

def study_B():
    w, h = 72, 30
    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
    img = np.zeros((h, w, 3), np.uint8)
    # behind: a farther bank in shadow, fine stipple (it is darker: the lit crest must read against it)
    img[:] = C["g_dol"]; t0 = rng.uniform(size=(h, w))
    img[t0 < 0.3] = C["g_dk"]; img[t0 > 0.9] = C["g_ochre"]
    yt = mound(w, h, 26, 36, 32, 13, rng)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w]
    inside = yy >= yt[None, :]
    t, d = mound_light(h, w, yt, 36)
    # family by stippled threshold: lit colour scattered into the shadow body
    lit = inside & (t > rng.uniform(size=(h, w)))
    Lmap = np.full((h, w), 1.0) + (yy / h) * 2.5
    grass_field(img, inside, lit, Lmap, rng)
    # crest band: the lightest tan dominates the 1-3 px under the contour on the lit part
    crest = inside & (d < 2) & (t > 0.8) & (rng.uniform(size=(h, w)) < 0.8)
    img[crest] = C["g_tan"]
    # blade tips poking above the silhouette, lit
    for x in range(w):
        if yt[x] < 10 ** 5 and rng.uniform() < 0.3 and xx[0, x] < 36 + 20:
            for k in range(1, rng.integers(2, 4)):
                if yt[x] - k >= 0: img[yt[x] - k, x] = C["g_tan"] if k < 3 else C["g_ochre"]
    sheet("B", "B  lit top of a grass mass: crest band of the lightest tan, lit colour stippled down into the shadow family, clean contour runs",
          [(photo_crop("refs/r20_grassbank_lake_sunset.jpg", (240, 420, 560, 580), 240), "photo r20 (tussock tops)"),
           (fer_crop((70, 205, 160, 240)), "Ferrari: bank crest at 8x"),
           (mine(img), "mine 8x")])
''' + "\n" + blk
open("translate_studies.py", "w").write(s)
EOF

64. pedagogy/observation/studies/translate_B.png

Bash: Add bank lip reflection study C
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("translate_studies.py").read()
blk = '''if __name__ == "__main__":
    for s in sys.argv[1:]: globals()["study_" + s]()
'''
s = s.replace(blk, "")
s += '''
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# C: the bank's lip and its reflection
# observed (r19, r20, r25): the grass ends in a dark line at the water (overhang shadow); the
# reflection hangs straight below, darker than the bank, shorter (we see it from above, the
# mirror shows the underside), simpler; beyond it the water carries the sky one step darker.
# Ferrari (island edge): a 1-px darkest line at the waterline; the reflection is built of
# 1-px HORIZONTAL runs in the bank's own hues shifted down one step; thin light wind-lines of
# sky colour cut across it.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
DARKER = {"g_tan": "g_mid", "g_mid": "g_ochre", "g_ochre": "g_dol", "g_gap": "g_blk", "g_dol": "g_dk",
          "g_dk": "g_blk", "g_blk": "g_blk", "sky1": "w_mid", "sky2": "w_mid"}

def reflect_rows(src_keys, y_line, n_rows, rng, compress=0.6, run=(2, 8)):
    """mirror the key-map rows above y_line into n_rows below it, compressed, shifted one value
    step darker, and resampled as horizontal runs"""
    h, w = src_keys.shape
    out = np.empty((n_rows, w), dtype=object)
    for r in range(n_rows):
        sy = int(round(y_line - 1 - r / compress))
        x = 0
        while x < w:
            L = int(rng.integers(run[0], run[1] + 1))
            k = src_keys[max(sy, 0), x] if sy >= 0 else "sky1"
            out[r, x:x + L] = DARKER.get(k, k)
            x += L
    return out

def study_C():
    w, h = 64, 34
    rng = np.random.default_rng(11)
    keys = np.full((h, w), "sky1", dtype=object)
    y_lip = 17
    # bank: a low lit mound falling toward the lip; body built with the B vocabulary
    yt = mound(w, h, y_lip, 22, 34, 9, rng, flat=0.25)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w]
    inside = (yy >= yt[None, :]) & (yy < y_lip)
    t, d = mound_light(h, w, yt, 22, falloff=8.0)
    lit = inside & (t > rng.uniform(size=(h, w)))
    r_ = rng.uniform(size=(h, w))
    keys[inside & lit] = "g_ochre"; keys[inside & lit & (r_ > 0.6)] = "g_tan"; keys[inside & lit & (r_ < 0.1)] = "g_gap"
    keys[inside & ~lit] = "g_dol"; keys[inside & ~lit & (r_ < 0.35)] = "g_dk"
    keys[inside & (d < 2) & (t > 0.7) & (r_ < 0.85)] = "g_tan"
    # far water above the bank (it is the tarn behind): sky reflection
    keys[(~inside) & (yy < y_lip)] = "w_mid"
    keys[(~inside) & (yy < y_lip) & (((xx + yy) & 1) == 1) & (yy < 8)] = "w_hi"
    # the lip: darkest line where grass overhangs water
    for x in range(w):
        if yt[x] < y_lip: keys[y_lip - 1, x] = "g_blk" if rng.uniform() < 0.85 else "g_dk"
    # reflection below, then the sky in the water (darker toward the viewer)
    nref = 9
    ref = reflect_rows(keys, y_lip, nref, rng)
    has_bank = yt < y_lip
    for r in range(nref):
        row = y_lip + r
        keys[row, has_bank] = ref[r, has_bank]
        keys[row, ~has_bank] = "w_mid"
    # sky reflection gradient: w_mid -> w_lo toward the viewer, checker strips between
    for row in range(y_lip, h):
        tt = (row - y_lip) / (h - y_lip)
        for x in range(w):
            if row >= y_lip + int(nref * 0.55 + 4 * (x % 7 == 0)) or not has_bank[x]:
                if row >= y_lip + nref or not has_bank[x]:
                    a, b = ("w_mid", "w_lo") if tt < 0.6 else ("w_lo", "w_dk")
                    f = (tt / 0.6) if tt < 0.6 else (tt - 0.6) / 0.4
                    keys[row, x] = b if (f > 2 / 3 or (f > 1 / 3 and (x + row) & 1)) else a
    # the reflection fades out into the sky-water with a ragged, horizontal-run edge
    for r in range(nref - 3, nref):
        row = y_lip + r
        for x in np.nonzero(has_bank)[0]:
            if rng.uniform() < (r - (nref - 4)) / 4: keys[row, x] = "w_lo"
    # wind lines: 1-px light streaks across the reflection and far water
    for _ in range(4):
        row = rng.integers(y_lip + 1, h - 2); x0 = rng.integers(0, w - 10); L = rng.integers(6, 18)
        keys[row, x0:x0 + L] = "w_hi" if row < y_lip + 6 else "w_mid"
    # overhanging blade tips at the lip, and their reflections as dark ticks
    for x in range(w):
        if has_bank[x] and rng.uniform() < 0.25:
            keys[y_lip - 1, x] = "g_ochre"
            if y_lip < h: keys[y_lip, x] = "g_ochre" if rng.uniform() < 0.5 else keys[y_lip, x]
    img = np.zeros((h, w, 3), np.uint8)
    for k in set(keys.ravel()): img[keys == k] = C[k]
    sheet("C", "C  bank lip and reflection: darkest line at the water, reflection = horizontal runs one step darker and shorter, wind-lines",
          [(photo_crop("refs/r19_tarn_bank_reflection.jpg", (420, 330, 960, 700), 272), "photo r19 (lip, boulder reflection)"),
           (fer_crop((296, 272, 400, 306)), "Ferrari: island edge + reflection 8x"),
           (mine(img), "mine 8x")])
''' + "\n" + blk
open("translate_studies.py", "w").write(s)
EOF
python3 translate_studies.py C 2>/dev/null

Bash: Add near blades against water study E
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("translate_studies.py").read()
blk = '''if __name__ == "__main__":
    for s in sys.argv[1:]: globals()["study_" + s]()
'''
s = s.replace(blk, "")
s += '''
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# E: near grass blades against bright water
# observed (r20, r19): where the bank's top meets the water, individual blades read against it;
# the mass behind them is dark; lit blades are light on the sun side.
# Ferrari (near-left grass over the tarn): blades are 1-px lines whose lean is made of EQUAL
# vertical runs (3 down, step 1 = a clean 3:1), tan when lit and dark-green in the gaps; tips
# thin out above the mass contour; at the mass top the tan is densest.
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def blade(img_keys, x0, y0, length, lean_run, direction, key_body, key_tip, tip_frac=0.45, allow=None):
    """a blade drawn as equal vertical runs: every lean_run px up, step 1 px sideways."""
    h, w = img_keys.shape
    x = x0
    for k in range(length):
        y = y0 - k
        if y < 0: break
        if lean_run > 0 and k > 0 and k % lean_run == 0: x += direction
        if x < 0 or x >= w: break
        if allow is not None and not allow[y, x]: break
        img_keys[y, x] = key_tip if k >= length * (1 - tip_frac) else key_body

def study_E():
    w, h = 48, 36
    rng = np.random.default_rng(5)
    keys = np.full((h, w), "w_mid", dtype=object)
    yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:h, 0:w]
    keys[(((xx + yy) & 1) == 1) & (yy < 10)] = "w_hi"
    keys[yy < 5] = "w_hi"
    # the bank mass: top contour from lower-left to right, clean runs
    xs = np.arange(w)
    yt = clean_profile(22 + 6 * np.sin(xs / 14.0 + 0.4) + 0.08 * xs)
    body = yy >= yt[None, :]
    r_ = rng.uniform(size=(h, w))
    keys[body] = "g_ochre"; keys[body & (r_ > 0.7)] = "g_tan"; keys[body & (r_ < 0.14)] = "g_gap"
    # blades rooted in the mass, far (upper) first; long ones rise past the contour
    roots = [(x, yt[x] + rng.integers(0, h - yt[x])) for x in range(w) for _ in range(3) if yt[x] < h]
    roots.sort(key=lambda p: p[1])
    for x, y in roots:
        if y >= h: continue
        near = (y - yt.min()) / max(1, h - yt.min())
        L = int(4 + 7 * near * rng.uniform(0.5, 1.2))
        gap = rng.uniform() < 0.3
        run = int(rng.choice([2, 3, 3, 4, 6]))
        blade(keys, x, y, L, run, -1 if rng.uniform() < 0.7 else 1,
              "g_gap" if gap else "g_ochre", "g_gap" if gap else "g_tan")
    img = np.zeros((h, w, 3), np.uint8)
    for k in set(keys.ravel()): img[keys == k] = C[k]
    sheet("E", "E  near blades against water: 1-px blades leaning in equal runs (clean 2:1 / 3:1), tan tips, dark-green gaps",
          [(photo_crop("refs/r20_grassbank_lake_sunset.jpg", (380, 400, 700, 560), 288), "photo r20 (blades vs water)"),
           (fer_crop((185, 345, 245, 381)), "Ferrari: near grass over the tarn 8x"),
           (mine(img), "mine 8x")])
''' + "\n" + blk
open("translate_studies.py", "w").write(s)
EOF

Write: pedagogy/observation/tarn.py
"""
tarn.py -- paint "a granite boulder resting in a grass bank at the edge of a still mountain tarn,
at dusk" as painterly pixel art in the manner of Mark Ferrari, from parameters.

    size      small | medium | large
    light     left | front | right | back
    distance  close | 10m | 40m
    seed      any int

Two stages (see LESSONS.md):

 1. OBSERVE  -- a small physical model of the scene, carrying what the photographs showed:
    low warm sun + violet sky fill + warm bounce from lit grass; granite's rough terminator; contact
    occlusion; long cast shadows at a 4.5 deg sun; tussock-scale grass masses that catch light on
    their sun-facing tops; water as a Fresnel mirror whose reflections are darker and show the
    underside of things; the far shore and mountains in dusk air.  Output: a G-buffer at 320x180.

 2. TRANSLATE -- the G-buffer is painted with the vocabulary found in the translation studies
    (translate_studies.py), never by quantising a smooth render:
      rock   continuous ramp coordinate with a JUMP at the terminator; flat planes; clustered
             dither only near value boundaries; vertical weathering grain; sun-side 1-px rim;
             darkest colour on the contact row                                   (study D, A)
      grass  two 3-colour families (lit: ochre/tan/dark-green; shadow: dark-olive/darker/black),
             family chosen per pixel by stippling the light amount; a band of the lightest tan
             under every sun-facing mass crest; blades as 1-px lines leaning in equal runs that
             shorten toward the tip; blade length ~ 1/distance                    (study B, E)
      water  the scene mirrored one value step darker, resampled as horizontal runs; checker
             for mirrored sky; light line at far waterlines, dark line at near lips; wind-lines
                                                                                  (study C)
      sky    flat bands of adjacent ramp colours joined by 50% checkerboard strips
      contours: mass tops and the shore are rasterised as consistent runs (flat / 2:1 / 45 deg)
"""
import os, sys, math, argparse
import numpy as np
from PIL import Image
from skimage.color import rgb2lab, lab2rgb
from pix import clean_profile

W, H = 320, 180
HFOV = math.radians(60.0)
PITCH = math.radians(-10.0)
EYE_H = 1.25
SUN_EL = math.radians(4.5)
SIZES = {"small": 1.1, "medium": 1.9, "large": 2.9}
DISTS = {"close": 5.5, "10m": 10.0, "40m": 40.0}
LIGHTS = {"left": -78.0, "front": 168.0, "right": 78.0, "back": -14.0}   # sun azimuth rel. to view

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# noise
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
M32 = np.uint64(0xFFFFFFFF)
def _hash(ix, iy, iz, seed):
    h = (ix.astype(np.int64).astype(np.uint64) * np.uint64(73856093)) ^ \
        (iy.astype(np.int64).astype(np.uint64) * np.uint64(19349663)) ^ \
        (iz.astype(np.int64).astype(np.uint64) * np.uint64(83492791)) ^ np.uint64((seed * 2654435761) & 0xFFFFFFFF)
    h &= M32; h ^= h >> np.uint64(13); h = (h * np.uint64(1274126177)) & M32; h ^= h >> np.uint64(16)
    return (h & np.uint64(0xFFFFFF)).astype(np.float64) / float(0xFFFFFF)

def vnoise3(x, y, z, seed=0):
    ix, iy, iz = np.floor(x), np.floor(y), np.floor(z)
    fx, fy, fz = x - ix, y - iy, z - iz
    ux, uy, uz = fx * fx * (3 - 2 * fx), fy * fy * (3 - 2 * fy), fz * fz * (3 - 2 * fz)
    r = 0.0
    for dx in (0, 1):
        for dy in (0, 1):
            for dz in (0, 1):
                w = (ux if dx else 1 - ux) * (uy if dy else 1 - uy) * (uz if dz else 1 - uz)
                r = r + w * _hash(ix + dx, iy + dy, iz + dz, seed)
    return r * 2 - 1

def fbm2(x, y, seed=0, oct=4):
    a, f, s = 1.0, 1.0, 0.0
    for o in range(oct):
        s = s + a * vnoise3(x * f, y * f, np.full_like(np.asarray(x, float), 0.5), seed + 17 * o); a *= 0.5; f *= 2.0
    return s

def fbm3(x, y, z, seed=0, oct=3):
    a, f, s = 1.0, 1.0, 0.0
    for o in range(oct):
        s = s + a * vnoise3(x * f, y * f, z * f, seed + 31 * o); a *= 0.5; f *= 2.0
    return s

def norm(v): return v / (np.linalg.norm(v, axis=-1, keepdims=True) + 1e-12)
def smoothstep(a, b, x):
    t = np.clip((x - a) / (b - a), 0, 1); return t * t * (3 - 2 * t)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# scene: geometry + light model (stage 1)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Scene:
    def __init__(self, size="medium", light="left", distance="10m", seed=0, tree_density=0.5):
        self.seed = seed; rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.size, self.light, self.distance = size, light, distance
        self.D = DISTS[distance]
        a = SIZES[size] / 2 * rng.uniform(0.9, 1.1); self.a = a
        self.half = a * np.array([1.0, rng.uniform(0.66, 0.82), rng.uniform(0.72, 0.9)])
        self.rr = 0.6 * self.half.min()
        self.byaw = rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8)
        self.sink = rng.uniform(0.22, 0.36)
        self.crack = (rng.uniform() < 0.6) and size != "small"
        self.crack_n = norm(np.array([rng.uniform(-1, 1), rng.uniform(-0.25, 0.25), rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5)]))
        self.crack_o = rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3) * a
        self.dents = [(norm(rng.normal(0, 1, 3)), rng.uniform(0.04, 0.09)) for _ in range(5)]
        # layout: camera on a near bank, looking across an inlet at the boulder standing on the
        # lip of a promontory; the tarn opens behind it to the far shore.
        ndc = 0.22 + rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05)
        self.bx = ndc * math.tan(HFOV / 2) * self.D
        self.bz = self.D
        self.z1 = max(3.0, 0.3 * self.D)                   # near bank lip
        self.wob = rng.uniform(0, 100)
        self.tarn_w = rng.uniform(70, 110)
        self.zfar = self.D + self.tarn_w
        self.tree_density = tree_density
        g = self.ground_h(np.array([self.bx]), np.array([self.bz]))[0]
        self.by = g + self.half[1] * (1 - 2 * self.sink)
        self.eye = np.array([0.0, self.ground_h(np.array([0.0]), np.array([0.0]))[0] + EYE_H, 0.0])
        self.yaw = rng.uniform(-0.03, 0.03)
        az = math.radians(LIGHTS[light]) + rng.uniform(-0.08, 0.08) + self.yaw
        self.sun = norm(np.array([math.sin(az) * math.cos(SUN_EL), math.sin(SUN_EL), math.cos(az) * math.cos(SUN_EL)]))
        self.back = (self.sun @ self.basis()[0]) > 0.3
        # light (linear rgb). observed: low sun warm-orange, sky fill violet, bounce warm
        self.sunC = np.array([1.00, 0.56, 0.30]) * 3.0
        self.skyC = np.array([0.36, 0.30, 0.52]) * 0.85
        self.grassA = np.array([0.30, 0.26, 0.10])
        self.rockA = np.array([0.46, 0.42, 0.38])
        self.bounceC = self.grassA * self.sunC * 0.28 + self.grassA * self.skyC * 0.5
        sh = np.array([self.sun[0], 0, self.sun[2]]); sh /= np.linalg.norm(sh) + 1e-9
        self.bfrom = norm(np.array([-sh[0] * 0.6, -1.0, -sh[2] * 0.6]))   # direction bounce light travels FROM (below, far side)

    # ---- terrain ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def bank_field(self, x, z):
        """signed horizontal distance to the shore (+ on land). land = near bank OR promontory."""
        a = self.a
        wob = 0.35 * fbm2(x * 0.25 + self.wob, z * 0.05, self.seed + 5, 3)
        # near bank: lip at z1, curving forward on the right to meet the promontory
        f1 = (self.z1 + wob * max(1.0, self.z1 * 0.15) - z) + 0.45 * np.maximum(0, x - (self.bx + 0.6 * a + 0.12 * self.D))
        # promontory: shore passes just left of the boulder's centre, recedes right behind it,
        # and runs forward-right toward the near bank
        u = x - self.bx; v = z - self.bz
        s = np.where(v < 0, -0.25 * a - 0.9 * v, -0.25 * a + 1.3 * v) + 0.2 * a * fbm2(v * 0.4 + self.wob, u * 0.0 + 1.3, self.seed + 6, 3)
        f2 = (u - s) / np.where(v < 0, 1.35, 1.64)
        return np.maximum(f1, f2)

    def ground_h(self, x, z, detail=True):
        d = self.bank_field(x, z)
        lip = 0.24
        bank = -0.05 + (lip + 0.05) * smoothstep(0.0, 0.3, d) + 0.04 * np.clip(d, 0, 30)
        if detail:
            t = fbm2(x * 0.8, z * 0.8, self.seed + 11, 3)
            t2 = fbm2(x * 2.7, z * 2.7, self.seed + 12, 2)
            bank = bank + (0.12 * t + 0.04 * t2) * smoothstep(0.1, 0.9, d)
        under = -0.05 + 0.35 * d
        return np.where(d >= 0, bank, np.maximum(under, -1.5))

    # ---- boulder ------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def local(self, p):
        c, s = math.cos(self.byaw), math.sin(self.byaw)
        q = p - np.array([self.bx, self.by, self.bz])
        return np.stack([c * q[..., 0] - s * q[..., 2], q[..., 1], s * q[..., 0] + c * q[..., 2]], -1)

    def boulder_sdf(self, p):
        L = self.local(p); a = self.a
        qq = np.abs(L) - (self.half - self.rr)
        d = np.linalg.norm(np.maximum(qq, 0), axis=-1) + np.minimum(qq.max(-1), 0) - self.rr
        for dirn, amp in self.dents:             # broad weathering dents (not pixel noise)
            c = dirn * self.half
            d = d + amp * a * np.exp(-np.sum((L - c) ** 2, -1) / (0.5 * a) ** 2)
        d = d + 0.02 * a * fbm3(L[..., 0] * 3 / a, L[..., 1] * 3 / a, L[..., 2] * 3 / a, self.seed + 22, 2)
        if self.crack:
            slab = np.abs(L @ self.crack_n - self.crack_o) - 0.02 * a
            d = np.maximum(d, -slab)
        return d

    def scene_sdf(self, p):
        return np.minimum((p[..., 1] - self.ground_h(p[..., 0], p[..., 2])) * 0.55, self.boulder_sdf(p))

    def material_at(self, p):
        return np.where(self.boulder_sdf(p) < (p[..., 1] - self.ground_h(p[..., 0], p[..., 2])), 2, 1)

    def normal(self, p, mat):
        e = 0.004 * max(self.a, 1.0); n = np.zeros_like(p)
        for i in range(3):
            dp = np.zeros(3); dp[i] = e
            n[..., i] = self.boulder_sdf(p + dp) - self.boulder_sdf(p - dp)
        hx = self.ground_h(p[..., 0] + 0.03, p[..., 2]) - self.ground_h(p[..., 0] - 0.03, p[..., 2])
        hz = self.ground_h(p[..., 0], p[..., 2] + 0.03) - self.ground_h(p[..., 0], p[..., 2] - 0.03)
        ng = np.stack([-hx, np.full_like(hx, 0.06), -hz], -1)
        return norm(np.where((mat == 2)[..., None], n, ng))

    def march(self, o, d, tmax, steps=260):
        t = np.full(d.shape[:-1], 0.02); hit = np.zeros(d.shape[:-1], bool); alive = np.ones_like(hit)
        for _ in range(steps):
            idx = np.nonzero(alive)
            if idx[0].size == 0: break
            p = o[idx] + d[idx] * t[idx][..., None]
            h = self.scene_sdf(p)
            h_hit = h < 0.0015 * t[idx] + 0.002
            hit[tuple(i[h_hit] for i in idx)] = True
            t[idx] = t[idx] + np.maximum(h, 0.004 + 0.002 * t[idx])
            done = h_hit | (t[idx] > tmax[idx])
            alive[tuple(i[done] for i in idx)] = False
        return t, hit & (t <= tmax + 1e-6)

    def soft_shadow(self, p, n):
        o = p + n * 0.03
        res = np.ones(p.shape[:-1]); t = np.full(p.shape[:-1], 0.05)
        for _ in range(80):
            h = self.scene_sdf(o + self.sun * t[..., None])
            res = np.minimum(res, 10.0 * h / t)
            t = t + np.clip(h, 0.04, 1.5)
            if np.all((t > 50) | (res < 0.02)): break
        return np.clip(res, 0, 1)

    def ao(self, p, n):
        occ = np.zeros(p.shape[:-1]); sc = 1.0; s = max(self.a, 0.8)
        for dd in (0.04, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.55):
            occ += (dd * s - self.scene_sdf(p + n * dd * s)) * sc; sc *= 0.7
        return np.clip(1 - 1.4 * occ / s, 0, 1)

    # ---- camera -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def basis(self):
        cy, sy, cp, sp = math.cos(self.yaw), math.sin(self.yaw), math.cos(PITCH), math.sin(PITCH)
        fwd = np.array([sy * cp, sp, cy * cp]); right = np.array([cy, 0, -sy]); up = np.cross(fwd, right)
        return fwd, right, (-up if up[1] < 0 else up)

    def rays(self):
        fwd, right, up = self.basis(); th = math.tan(HFOV / 2); tv = th * H / W
        X, Y = np.meshgrid(((np.arange(W) + 0.5) / W * 2 - 1) * th, (1 - (np.arange(H) + 0.5) / H * 2) * tv)
        return norm(fwd + X[..., None] * right + Y[..., None] * up)

    def project(self, pts, is_dir=False):
        fwd, right, up = self.basis(); v = pts if is_dir else pts - self.eye
        zc = v @ fwd; th = math.tan(HFOV / 2); tv = th * H / W
        return (v @ right / zc / th + 1) / 2 * W - 0.5, (1 - v @ up / zc / tv) / 2 * H - 0.5, zc

    # ---- sky & far ----------------------------------------------------------------------
    def sky(self, d):
        el = np.arcsin(np.clip(d[..., 1], -1, 1)); e = np.clip(el, 0, None)
        cosg = np.clip(d @ self.sun, -1, 1)
        zen = np.array([0.30, 0.24, 0.48]); mid = np.array([0.62, 0.40, 0.58]); hor = np.array([0.95, 0.56, 0.48])
        t1 = smoothstep(0.0, 0.42, e)[..., None]; base = hor * (1 - t1) + mid * t1
        t2 = smoothstep(0.12, 0.9, e)[..., None]; base = base * (1 - t2) + zen * t2
        glow = np.exp(-(1 - cosg) / 0.10)[..., None] * np.array([1.0, 0.62, 0.30]) * 1.4 * np.exp(-e / 0.25)[..., None]
        halo = np.exp(-(1 - cosg) / 0.6)[..., None] * np.array([0.35, 0.18, 0.06]) * np.exp(-e / 0.4)[..., None]
        belt = np.exp(-((el - 0.10) / 0.07) ** 2)[..., None] * smoothstep(0.2, -0.6, cosg)[..., None] * np.array([0.10, 0.02, 0.06])
        disk = (cosg > math.cos(math.radians(0.9)))[..., None] * np.array([3.0, 2.3, 1.2])
        return (base + glow + halo + belt) * 0.62 + disk

    def mountain_ridge(self, az):
        r = 0.045 + 0.055 * (0.5 + 0.5 * fbm2(az * 3.0 + self.seed * 0.37, np.zeros_like(az) + 1.7, self.seed + 40, 5))
        return r + 0.03 * np.maximum(0, fbm2(az * 1.3 + 9.1, np.zeros_like(az), self.seed + 41, 3))

    def far_layer(self, d):
        """sky + distant granite range (3 km, dusk air), by direction. -> linear rgb, mountain mask, lit mask"""
        az = np.arctan2(d[..., 0], d[..., 2]); el = np.arcsin(np.clip(d[..., 1], -1, 1))
        ridge = self.mountain_ridge(az); mnt = el < ridge
        def R(a, e):
            return (1 - np.abs(fbm2(a * 9 + 3.1, e * 14 + 1.3, self.seed + 43, 4))) * 0.6 + (ridge - e) * 3
        ea = 0.0015
        gx = (R(az + ea, el) - R(az - ea, el)) / (2 * ea) * 0.012
        gy = (R(az, el + ea) - R(az, el - ea)) / (2 * ea) * 0.012
        vd = norm(np.stack([d[..., 0], np.zeros_like(el), d[..., 2]], -1))
        side = np.stack([vd[..., 2], np.zeros_like(el), -vd[..., 0]], -1)
        n = norm(-vd + side * (-gx)[..., None] * 1.3 + np.array([0, 1, 0]) * (0.45 - 0.4 * gy)[..., None])
        ndl = np.clip(n @ self.sun, 0, 1) ** 0.7
        snow = (fbm2(az * 22, el * 30, self.seed + 44, 3) + 0.9 * (el - ridge + 0.04) / 0.04 > 0.55) & (n[..., 1] > 0.35)
        alb = np.where(snow[..., None], np.array([0.80, 0.78, 0.86]), np.array([0.34, 0.30, 0.32]))
        col = alb * (self.sunC * 0.55 * ndl[..., None] + self.skyC * 1.3 * (0.5 + 0.5 * n[..., 1:2]))
        hz = self.sky(norm(np.stack([d[..., 0], np.full_like(el, 0.02), d[..., 2]], -1)))
        col = col * 0.5 + hz * 0.5
        return np.where(mnt[..., None], col, self.sky(d)), mnt, mnt & (ndl > 0.35)

    def mid_layer(self):
        """far shore bank + conifers as marks on an extended canvas (rows -OFF..H).
        keys: 1 bank lit, 7 bank shade, 2 tree dark, 3 tree lit edge, 4 tree mid"""
        OFF = 150; HH = H + OFF
        mark = np.zeros((HH, W), np.int8)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + 99)
        zf = self.zfar; rays = self.rays()[0]
        rows = []
        for zz, hh in ((zf, 0.0), (zf + 30, 3.0)):
            pts = np.array([self.eye + rays[i] * ((zz - self.eye[2]) / rays[i][2]) for i in range(W)]); pts[:, 1] = hh
            rows.append(self.project(pts)[1])
        y_wl, y_top = rows
        bn = fbm2(np.arange(W) * 0.05 + self.seed, np.zeros(W), self.seed + 60, 3) * 1.5
        yt = clean_profile(y_top + bn)
        for x in range(W):
            y0 = int(round(y_wl[x])) + OFF; y1 = int(yt[x]) + OFF
            y0 = min(max(y0, 0), HH - 1); y1 = min(max(y1, 0), y0)
            mark[y1:y0 + 1, x] = 1
        # shading of the far bank: lit if the sun is not behind it
        fwd, right, up = self.basis()
        if self.back: mark[mark == 1] = 7
        dist = zf + 12 - self.eye[2]; pix_ang = HFOV / W
        xs_t = rng.uniform(-30, W + 30, int(W / 5 * self.tree_density))
        keep = fbm2(xs_t * 0.02 + self.seed * 0.1, np.zeros_like(xs_t), self.seed + 61, 2) > -0.1
        xs_t = np.sort(xs_t[keep])
        sunx = self.sun @ right
        for x0 in xs_t:
            hm = rng.uniform(7, 18) * (1 if rng.uniform() > 0.25 else 0.5)
            hp = hm / dist / pix_ang
            base = int(round(np.interp(x0, np.arange(W), yt))) + OFF + int(rng.integers(0, 3))
            wmax = max(1.5, hp * rng.uniform(0.2, 0.28)); tier = max(2, int(round(hp / rng.uniform(5, 8))))
            for r in range(int(hp)):
                y = base - int(hp) + r
                if y < 0 or y >= HH: continue
                f = r / hp; ph = (r % tier) / tier
                hw = wmax * f * (0.5 + 0.5 * ph) + (0.5 if r > 1 else 0)
                j = rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6)
                xa = int(round(x0 - hw + j)); xb = int(round(x0 + hw + j))
                for x in range(max(xa, 0), min(xb, W - 1) + 1):
                    m = 2
                    edge_sun = (x == xb and sunx > 0.2) or (x == xa and sunx < -0.2)
                    if edge_sun and ph > 0.25: m = 3
                    elif rng.uniform() < 0.15: m = 4
                    if self.back and (x in (xa, xb)) and ph > 0.5 and rng.uniform() < 0.5: m = 3
                    mark[y, x] = m
        return mark, OFF

    # ---- shading --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def shade(self, p, mat, n, vdir):
        vis = self.soft_shadow(p, n); ao = self.ao(p, n)
        ndl = np.clip(n @ self.sun, 0, 1); rock = mat == 2
        sunk = np.where(rock, ndl ** 0.55, 0.22 + 1.05 * ndl) * vis
        skyk = (0.55 + 0.45 * n[..., 1]) * ao
        bnc = np.clip(n @ (-self.bfrom), 0, 1) * ao
        alb = np.where(rock[..., None], self.rockA, self.grassA)
        trans = np.where(rock, 0, np.clip(-(vdir @ self.sun), 0, 1) ** 3 * vis * 0.9)
        col = alb * (self.sunC * (sunk + trans)[..., None] + self.skyC * skyk[..., None] + self.bounceC * bnc[..., None])
        return col, dict(vis=vis, ao=ao, ndl=ndl, trans=trans, bnc=np.clip(n @ (-self.bfrom), 0, 1))

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
SKY, GRASS, ROCK, WATER, FAR, MID = 0, 1, 2, 3, 4, 5

def observe(sc):
    d = sc.rays(); o = sc.eye
    tw = np.where(d[..., 1] < -1e-6, (0 - o[1]) / np.minimum(d[..., 1], -1e-6), np.inf)
    tfar = np.where(d[..., 2] > 1e-6, (sc.zfar - o[2]) / np.maximum(d[..., 2], 1e-6), np.inf)
    tmax = np.minimum(np.minimum(tw, tfar), 400.0)
    t, hit = sc.march(np.broadcast_to(o, d.shape).copy(), d, tmax)
    p = o + d * t[..., None]
    G = {k: np.zeros((H, W)) for k in ("vis", "ao", "ndl", "trans", "bnc")}
    mat = np.full((H, W), SKY, np.int8)
    water = (~hit) & (tw < tfar); mat[water] = WATER
    mat[hit] = sc.material_at(p)[hit]
    depth = np.where(hit, t, np.where(water, tw, 1e4))
    nrm = np.zeros((H, W, 3)); col = np.zeros((H, W, 3)); pos = p.copy()
    if hit.any():
        n = sc.normal(p[hit], mat[hit]); c, inf = sc.shade(p[hit], mat[hit], n, d[hit])
        nrm[hit] = n; col[hit] = c
        for k in G: G[k][hit] = inf[k]
    mark, OFF = sc.mid_layer()
    farc, mnt, mlit = sc.far_layer(d)
    far = (~hit) & (~water)
    col[far] = farc[far]; mat[far & mnt] = FAR
    midm = mark[OFF:OFF + H]
    mid_here = (midm > 0) & far
    mat[mid_here] = MID
    # water: rippled Fresnel mirror
    R = np.zeros((H, W)); refl = {"mat": np.full((H, W), -1, np.int8), "col": np.zeros((H, W, 3)),
                                  "key": np.zeros((H, W), np.int8), "mlit": np.zeros((H, W), bool),
                                  "vis": np.zeros((H, W)), "ndl": np.zeros((H, W)), "ao": np.ones((H, W)),
                                  "nrm": np.zeros((H, W, 3)), "hgt": np.zeros((H, W)), "bnc": np.zeros((H, W))}
    if water.any():
        yy, xx = np.nonzero(water)
        pw = o + d[water] * tw[water][..., None]
        rng = np.random.default_rng(sc.seed + 5)
        rip = fbm2(xx * 0.05 + sc.seed, yy * 0.8, sc.seed + 80, 3)       # long in x, short in y
        amp = math.radians(0.3)
        nw = norm(np.stack([np.zeros_like(rip), np.ones_like(rip), rip * amp * 2.0], -1))
        # wind lines: sparse 1-px horizontal segments tilted so they see higher (lighter) sky
        wmask = np.zeros((H, W)); ys_w = np.nonzero(water.any(1))[0]
        for _ in range(int(len(ys_w) * 0.35) + 2):
            yrow = rng.choice(ys_w); x0 = int(rng.integers(0, W)); L = int(rng.integers(5, 40))
            wmask[yrow, x0:x0 + L] = 1
        wl = wmask[yy, xx]
        nw = norm(nw + np.stack([np.zeros_like(wl), np.zeros_like(wl), -wl * math.radians(2.5)], -1))
        dv = d[water]
        dr = norm(dv - 2 * np.sum(dv * nw, -1)[..., None] * nw)
        tfr = np.where(dr[:, 2] > 1e-6, (sc.zfar - pw[:, 2]) / np.maximum(dr[:, 2], 1e-6), np.inf)
        tr, hr = sc.march(pw + dr * 0.01, dr, np.minimum(200.0, tfr), steps=200)
        rmat = np.full(len(yy), -1, np.int8); rc = np.zeros((len(yy), 3)); rkey = np.zeros(len(yy), np.int8)
        rml = np.zeros(len(yy), bool)
        rG = {k: np.zeros(len(yy)) for k in ("vis", "ndl", "ao", "bnc", "hgt")}; rG["ao"][:] = 1
        rn = np.zeros((len(yy), 3))
        if hr.any():
            ph = pw[hr] + dr[hr] * tr[hr][..., None]; mh = sc.material_at(ph); nh = sc.normal(ph, mh)
            ch, inf = sc.shade(ph, mh, nh, dr[hr])
            rc[hr] = ch; rmat[hr] = mh; rn[hr] = nh
            for k in ("vis", "ndl", "ao", "bnc"): rG[k][hr] = inf[k]
            rG["hgt"][hr] = ph[:, 1] - sc.ground_h(ph[:, 0], ph[:, 2])
        miss = ~hr
        if miss.any():
            q = pw[miss] + dr[miss] * np.minimum(tfr[miss], 1e5)[..., None]
            qpx, qpy, qz = sc.project(q)
            qx = np.clip(np.round(qpx).astype(int), 0, W - 1); qy = np.round(qpy).astype(int) + OFF
            ok = (qy >= 0) & (qy < H + OFF) & np.isfinite(tfr[miss]) & (qz > 0)
            qy = np.clip(qy, 0, H + OFF - 1)
            midhit = ok & (mark[qy, qx] > 0)
            fc, fm, fl = sc.far_layer(dr[miss])
            rc[miss] = fc; rkey[miss] = np.where(midhit, mark[qy, qx], 0)
            rmat[miss] = np.where(midhit, MID, np.where(fm, FAR, SKY)); rml[miss] = fl
        cos_i = np.clip(-np.sum(dv * nw, -1), 0, 1)
        Rf = 0.02 + 0.98 * (1 - cos_i) ** 5
        R[water] = Rf
        refl["mat"][water] = rmat; refl["col"][water] = rc; refl["key"][water] = rkey; refl["mlit"][water] = rml
        for k in rG: refl[k][water] = rG[k]
        refl["nrm"][water] = rn
    hgt = np.zeros((H, W))
    if hit.any(): hgt[hit] = p[hit][:, 1] - sc.ground_h(p[hit][:, 0], p[hit][:, 2])
    return dict(col=col, mat=mat, depth=depth, nrm=nrm, pos=pos, R=R, refl=refl, midm=midm,
                farlit=mlit, hgt=hgt, **G)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# palette (32): every entry is the light model evaluated at a canonical light state, or a
# k-means centre of the observed sky / mountains.
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def tone(c):
    c = np.maximum(c, 0); c = c / (1 + 0.35 * c)
    return np.where(c <= 0.0031308, 12.92 * c, 1.055 * np.power(np.clip(c, 0, None), 1 / 2.4) - 0.055).clip(0, 1)

def to_lab(srgb): return rgb2lab(np.asarray(srgb, float).reshape(-1, 1, 3)).reshape(np.shape(srgb))

def kmeans_ramp(samples, k, seed=0):
    from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
    lab = to_lab(samples); k = min(k, len(np.unique(np.round(lab), axis=0)))
    km = KMeans(k, n_init=3, random_state=seed).fit(lab)
    cen = km.cluster_centers_[np.argsort(km.cluster_centers_[:, 0])]
    return lab2rgb(cen.reshape(-1, 1, 3)).reshape(-1, 3).clip(0, 1)

class Palette:
    """named entries. grass families and rock zones come from canonical light states."""
    def __init__(self, sc, obs):
        s = sc; P = {}
        gA = s.grassA; dry = gA * np.array([1.15, 1.0, 0.7]); green = gA * np.array([0.6, 1.0, 0.8])
        sun, sky, bnc = s.sunC, s.skyC, s.bounceC
        # grass lit family (ochre base / tan blade / dark-green gap) + the crest tan
        P["g_crest"] = tone(dry * (sun * 1.30 + sky))
        P["g_tan"] = tone(dry * (sun * 0.80 + sky))
        P["g_ochre"] = tone(dry * (sun * 0.34 + sky * 0.9))
        P["g_gap"] = tone(green * (sun * 0.03 + sky * 0.30))
        # grass shadow family (dark olive base / darker blade / near-black gap)
        P["g_dol"] = tone(gA * (sky * 0.95 + bnc * 0.3))
        P["g_dk"] = tone(gA * (sky * 0.45))
        P["g_blk"] = tone(gA * sky * 0.10)
        P["g_mid"] = tone(dry * (sun * 0.14 + sky * 0.95))          # transition / reflections
        # rock zones: contact / core / bounce-lit shadow / half (sky-lit top) / lit / rim
        rA = s.rockA
        P["r_contact"] = tone(rA * sky * 0.12)
        P["r_core"] = tone(rA * (sky * 0.38 + bnc * 0.1))
        P["r_shade"] = tone(rA * (sky * 0.62 + bnc * 0.9))
        P["r_half"] = tone(rA * (sky * 1.05 + sun * 0.16))
        P["r_lit"] = tone(rA * (sky * 0.8 + sun * 0.62))
        P["r_rim"] = tone(rA * (sky * 0.8 + sun * 1.05))
        # sky ramp (8) from the observed sky incl. the part the near water mirrors
        az = np.linspace(-0.6, 0.6, 30) + s.yaw; el = np.linspace(0.0, 0.7, 40)
        A, E = np.meshgrid(az, el)
        dd = np.stack([np.sin(A) * np.cos(E), np.sin(E), np.cos(A) * np.cos(E)], -1).reshape(-1, 3)
        sk = s.sky(dd); sk = sk[(dd @ s.sun) < math.cos(math.radians(1.5))]
        mat = obs["mat"]; skypx = obs["col"][mat == SKY]
        self.sky = kmeans_ramp(tone(np.concatenate([skypx, sk])), 8, s.seed)
        farpx = obs["col"][mat == FAR]
        if len(farpx) < 16: farpx = s.far_layer(dd[:400] * np.array([1, 0.05, 1]))[0]
        self.far = kmeans_ramp(tone(farpx), 4, s.seed)
        # water: the sky ramp mirrored one and two steps darker, and the deep body
        self.water = np.array([tone(self._lin(self.sky[4]) * 0.55), tone(self._lin(self.sky[2]) * 0.42),
                               tone(self._lin(self.sky[1]) * 0.28), tone(self._lin(self.sky[0]) * 0.16)])
        P["accent"] = tone(sun * 0.9) if s.back else tone(dry * (sun * 1.8 + sky))
        self.named = P
        names = list(P.keys())
        cols = [P[n] for n in names] + list(self.sky) + list(self.far) + list(self.water)
        self.names = names + [f"sky{i}" for i in range(len(self.sky))] + [f"far{i}" for i in range(len(self.far))] + [f"w{i}" for i in range(4)]
        self.rgb = np.clip(np.array(cols), 0, 1)
        assert len(self.rgb) <= 32, len(self.rgb)
        self.lab = to_lab(self.rgb)
        self.i = {n: k for k, n in enumerate(self.names)}

    @staticmethod
    def _lin(c):
        c = np.asarray(c, float); return np.where(c <= 0.04045, c / 12.92, ((c + 0.055) / 1.055) ** 2.4)

    def ids(self, prefix): return np.array([self.i[n] for n in self.names if n.startswith(prefix)])

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# stage 2: translation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
class Painter:
    def __init__(self, sc, obs, pal):
        self.sc, self.o, self.pal = sc, obs, pal
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(sc.seed + 777)
        self.img = np.zeros((H, W), np.int16)
        yy, xx = np.mgrid[0:H, 0:W]; self.yy, self.xx = yy, xx
        self.chk = ((xx + yy) & 1) == 1

    def I(self, name): return self.pal.i[name]

    # -- helpers ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def ramp_pos(self, lab, ids):
        R = self.pal.lab[ids]; best = np.full(lab.shape[:-1], np.inf); pos = np.zeros(lab.shape[:-1])
        for i in range(len(R) - 1):
            a, b = R[i], R[i + 1]; ab = b - a
            t = np.clip(((lab - a) @ ab) / (ab @ ab + 1e-9), 0, 1)
            dd = np.linalg.norm(lab - (a + t[..., None] * ab), axis=-1)
            m = dd < best; best[m] = dd[m]; pos[m] = i + t[m]
        return pos

    def checker3(self, f):
        return np.where(f < 1 / 3, False, np.where(f > 2 / 3, True, self.chk))

    def clustered(self):
        h, w = H, W; r = self.rng
        c = np.repeat(np.repeat(r.uniform(size=(h // 2 + 2, w // 2 + 2)), 2, 0), 2, 1)[:h, :w]
        off = r.integers(0, 2, size=h)
        for y in range(h): c[y] = np.roll(c[y], off[y])
        return 0.55 * c + 0.45 * r.uniform(size=(h, w))

    def hruns(self, lo=2, hi=7):
        run = np.zeros((H, W))
        for y in range(H):
            x = 0
            while x < W:
                L = int(self.rng.integers(lo, hi + 1)); run[y, x:x + L] = self.rng.uniform(); x += L
        return run

    # -- the painting --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    def paint(self):
        o = self.o; mat = o["mat"]
        lab = to_lab(tone(o["col"]))
        # sky: bands + 50% checker strips
        sky_ids = self.pal.ids("sky")
        pos = self.ramp_pos(lab, sky_ids)
        i0 = np.clip(np.floor(pos).astype(int), 0, len(sky_ids) - 1); i1 = np.clip(i0 + 1, 0, len(sky_ids) - 1)
        pick = np.where(self.checker3(pos - np.floor(pos)), sky_ids[i1], sky_ids[i0])
        self.img[mat == SKY] = pick[mat == SKY]
        if self.sc.back:
            sun = (mat == SKY) & (tone(o["col"]).sum(-1) > 2.75); self.img[sun] = self.I("accent")
        # mountains: facets. ramp + clustered dither, lit ridges get the lightest far colour
        far_ids = self.pal.ids("far"); pos = self.ramp_pos(lab, far_ids)
        i0 = np.clip(np.floor(pos).astype(int), 0, 3); i1 = np.clip(i0 + 1, 0, 3)
        f = pos - np.floor(pos); th = self.clustered()
        fm = mat == FAR
        self.img[fm] = np.where(np.clip((f - 0.5) * 1.8 + 0.5, 0, 1) > th, far_ids[i1], far_ids[i0])[fm]
        self.paint_mid(self.o["midm"], mat == MID)
        self.paint_water()
        self.paint_rock()
        self.paint_grass()
        return self.img

    def mid_key_to_idx(self, key, shape):
        r = self.rng.uniform(size=shape)
        out = np.full(shape, self.I("g_dk"))
        out = np.where(key == 1, np.where(r < 0.62, self.I("g_ochre"), np.where(r < 0.82, self.I("g_mid"), self.I("g_dol"))), out)
        out = np.where(key == 7, np.where(r < 0.6, self.I("g_dol"), self.I("g_dk")), out)
        out = np.where(key == 2, np.where(r < 0.7, self.I("g_blk"), self.I("g_dk")), out)
        out = np.where(key == 3, self.I("g_tan") if not self.sc.back else self.I("g_crest"), out)
        out = np.where(key == 4, self.I("g_dol"), out)
        return out

    def paint_mid(self, key, m):
        idx = self.mid_key_to_idx(key, key.shape)
        self.img[m] = idx[m]

    # -- rock (study D, A) -----------------------------------------------------------------
    def rock_value(self, n, vis, ao, bnc, hgt, ndl_sun):
        """ramp coordinate 1 contact .. 2 core .. 3 shade .. 4 half .. 5 lit (6 rim is placed as an
        edge mark). The terminator is a jump: lit side and shadow side are separate ranges."""
        sc = self.sc
        lit_amt = ndl_sun * vis
        lit = 3.75 + 1.25 * np.clip((lit_amt - 0.12) / 0.45, 0, 1) ** 0.7
        shd = 2.0 + 1.0 * np.clip((bnc - 0.3) / 0.35, 0, 1)
        shd = np.maximum(shd, 2.0 + 2.0 * np.clip((n[..., 1] - 0.45) / 0.4, 0, 1) * 0.95)   # sky-lit tops
        v = np.where(lit_amt > 0.12, np.maximum(lit, shd), shd)
        occ = np.clip(1 - (hgt / (0.3 * sc.a + 1e-6)), 0, 1) * 0.8 + (1 - ao) * 1.2
        v = v - np.clip(occ, 0, 1) * (v - 1.0) * 0.8
        return v

    def paint_rock(self):
        o = self.o; m = o["mat"] == ROCK
        if not m.any(): return
        ids = [self.I(k) for k in ("r_contact", "r_contact", "r_core", "r_shade", "r_half", "r_lit", "r_rim")]
        v = self.rock_value(o["nrm"], o["vis"], o["ao"], o["bnc"], o["hgt"], o["ndl"])
        npx = m.sum(); scale = np.sqrt(npx / 3000.0)
        # vertical weathering grain (only where the rock is big enough to carry texture)
        g = self.rng.uniform(-1, 1, (H // 3 + 2, W)); g = np.repeat(g, 3, 0)[:H]
        g = 0.5 * g + 0.5 * np.roll(g, 1, 0)
        v = v + 0.33 * min(1.0, scale) * g
        lo = np.floor(v); f = v - lo
        z = np.where(np.clip((f - 0.5) * 2.2 + 0.5, 0, 1) > self.clustered(), lo + 1, lo).astype(int)
        z = np.clip(z, 1, 5)
        # rim: silhouette pixels on the sun side that face the sun
        fwd, right, up = self.sc.basis()
        sx = self.sc.sun @ right
        dx = -1 if sx < -0.2 else (1 if sx > 0.2 else 0)
        out_sun = ~np.roll(m, -dx, 1) if dx else np.zeros_like(m)
        out_up = ~np.roll(m, 1, 0)
        rim = m & (out_sun | out_up) & (o["ndl"] * o["vis"] > 0.3)
        if self.sc.back:
            rim |= m & out_up & (o["nrm"][..., 1] > 0.25)       # back-light: the top edge glows
        z[rim] = 6
        # contact: lowest boulder pixel in each column touching grass -> darkest
        below = np.roll(o["mat"], -1, 0)
        z[m & (below == GRASS)] = 1
        idx = np.array(ids)[z]
        self.img[m] = idx[m]

    # -- grass (study B, E, A) ---------------------------------------------------------------
    def grass_light(self):
        o = self.o
        t = np.clip(o["vis"] * (0.1 + 1.1 * o["ndl"]) / 0.75, 0, 1)
        t = np.maximum(t, np.clip(o["trans"] * 1.3, 0, 1))
        return t

    def paint_grass(self):
        o = self.o; sc = self.sc; mat = o["mat"]; m = mat == GRASS
        if not m.any(): return
        rng = self.rng; depth = o["depth"]
        t = self.grass_light()
        lit = m & (t > rng.uniform(size=(H, W)))
        I = self.I
        # blade length (px): 0.3 m blades projected; taller at the boulder's foot and the lip
        pix_ang = HFOV / W
        P = o["pos"]
        foot = np.clip(1 - sc.boulder_sdf(P) / (0.4 * sc.a + 0.15), 0, 1) * m
        lipd = sc.bank_field(P[..., 0], P[..., 2])
        lipz = np.clip(1 - lipd / 0.5, 0, 1) * m
        L = 0.3 / np.maximum(depth, 0.1) / pix_ang * 0.5 * (1 + 1.0 * foot + 0.7 * lipz)
        L = np.clip(L, 0, 13)
        # base fill: family base colours, stipple (the far grass is only this)
        r = rng.uniform(size=(H, W))
        base = np.where(lit, I("g_ochre"), I("g_dol"))
        base = np.where(lit & (r > 0.84), I("g_tan"), base)
        base = np.where(~lit & (r < 0.35), I("g_dk"), base)
        far = L < 2.5
        base = np.where(far & lit & (r < 0.07), I("g_gap"), base)
        base = np.where(far & ~lit & (r > 0.93), I("g_blk"), base)
        self.img[m] = base[m]
        # crest bands: grass pixels whose upper neighbour is much farther (a mass top against the
        # land/water behind it) and that face the sun get 1-3 px of the lightest tan
        up_depth = np.roll(depth, 1, 0); up_mat = np.roll(mat, 1, 0)
        top = m & ((up_mat != GRASS) | (up_depth > depth * 1.12 + 0.3))
        top[0] = False
        tl = top & (t > 0.45)
        bandw = np.clip(0.8 + 6.0 / np.maximum(depth, 1.0), 1, 3).astype(int)
        for k in range(3):
            sel = np.roll(tl & (bandw > k), k, 0) & m & lit
            if k == 0: sel = np.roll(tl, 0, 0)
            keep = rng.uniform(size=(H, W)) < (0.9 - 0.2 * k)
            self.img[sel & keep] = I("g_crest") if k == 0 else I("g_tan")
        # blades, far to near, z-tested
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(m & (L >= 1.8))
        order = np.argsort(depth[ys, xs])[::-1]
        img = self.img; wind = -1 if rng.uniform() < 0.6 else 1
        for y, x in zip(ys[order], xs[order]):
            Lp = L[y, x]
            if rng.uniform() > min(1.0, 1.7 / Lp + 0.1): continue
            fam_lit = lit[y, x]
            gap = rng.uniform() < 0.3 and Lp >= 3
            n = max(2, int(round(Lp * rng.uniform(0.6, 1.2))))
            zr = depth[y, x]
            if gap:
                n = max(2, n // 2); run = 0; c_body = c_tip = I("g_gap") if fam_lit else I("g_blk")
            else:
                run = int(rng.choice([2, 3, 3, 4, 5, 6]))
                if fam_lit:
                    c_body = I("g_tan") if rng.uniform() < 0.5 else I("g_ochre"); c_tip = I("g_tan")
                    if t[y, x] > 0.85 and rng.uniform() < 0.4: c_tip = I("g_crest")
                else:
                    c_body = I("g_dk"); c_tip = I("g_dol")
            direc = wind if rng.uniform() < 0.75 else -wind
            xx = x; posr = 0
            for k in range(n):
                yy = y - k
                if yy < 0: break
                if k > 0:
                    posr += 1
                    if run > 0 and posr >= run:
                        xx += direc; posr = 0
                        if k > n * 0.35: run = max(1, run - 1)
                if xx < 0 or xx >= W: break
                if depth[yy, xx] < zr - 0.05 and mat[yy, xx] != GRASS: break
                if mat[yy, xx] == GRASS and depth[yy, xx] < zr - 0.25: break
                img[yy, xx] = c_tip if k >= n * 0.45 else c_body

    # -- water (study C) -----------------------------------------------------------------------
    def paint_water(self):
        o = self.o; sc = self.sc; m = o["mat"] == WATER
        if not m.any(): return
        rf = o["refl"]; rmat = rf["mat"]; I = self.I; rng = self.rng
        Reff = np.clip(0.3 + 0.9 * o["R"], 0, 1)          # observed: near water darker, not black
        # 1) mirrored sky: continuous * R -> sky+water ramps, checker (smooth)
        c = tone(rf["col"] * Reff[..., None] + np.array([0.02, 0.016, 0.03]) * (1 - Reff[..., None]))
        lab = to_lab(c)
        cand = np.concatenate([self.pal.ids("sky"), self.pal.ids("w")])
        cand = cand[np.argsort(self.pal.lab[cand][:, 0])]
        pos = self.ramp_pos(lab, cand)
        i0 = np.clip(np.floor(pos).astype(int), 0, len(cand) - 1); i1 = np.clip(i0 + 1, 0, len(cand) - 1)
        sky_pick = np.where(self.checker3(pos - np.floor(pos)), cand[i1], cand[i0])
        # 2) mirrored things: paint them in their own vocabulary one step darker, resampled as
        #    horizontal runs (reflections read as horizontal dashes)
        run = self.hruns(2, 7)
        thing = np.full((H, W), -1)
        r = rng.uniform(size=(H, W))
        # grass: reflect the families one step down
        gl = (rmat == GRASS)
        tl = np.clip(rf["vis"] * (0.1 + 1.1 * rf["ndl"]) / 0.75, 0, 1)
        litr = tl > run
        thing = np.where(gl & litr, np.where(r < 0.8, I("g_ochre"), I("g_mid")), thing)
        thing = np.where(gl & ~litr, np.where(run < 0.5, I("g_dk"), I("g_blk")), thing)
        # rock: the mirror sees the underside -> mostly core/shade, lit faces one step down
        rl = rmat == ROCK
        v = self.rock_value(rf["nrm"], rf["vis"], rf["ao"], rf["bnc"], rf["hgt"], rf["ndl"]) - 1.0
        rock_ids = np.array([I(k) for k in ("r_contact", "r_contact", "r_core", "r_shade", "r_half", "r_lit", "r_rim")])
        vz = np.clip(np.floor(v + run * 0.8), 1, 5).astype(int)
        thing = np.where(rl, rock_ids[vz], thing)
        # far bank / trees
        ml = rmat == MID
        mk = rf["key"]
        down = {1: I("g_mid"), 7: I("g_dk"), 2: I("g_blk"), 3: I("g_ochre"), 4: I("g_dk")}
        for k, vv in down.items():
            thing = np.where(ml & (mk == k), vv, thing)
        # mountains: far ramp one step down
        fl = rmat == FAR
        far_ids = self.pal.ids("far")
        fpos = self.ramp_pos(to_lab(tone(rf["col"] * 0.7)), far_ids)
        fz = np.clip(np.floor(fpos + run * 0.9).astype(int), 0, 3)
        thing = np.where(fl, far_ids[fz], thing)
        # Fresnel: toward the viewer the reflection sinks into the water body -> dither into w3
        deep = Reff < 0.55
        thing = np.where((thing >= 0) & deep & (run > (Reff - 0.3) / 0.25), I("w3"), thing)
        out = np.where(thing >= 0, thing, sky_pick)
        self.img[m] = out[m]
        # 3) waterlines: first water pixel under something. far shore -> light line; near lip or
        #    rock foot -> dark line (overhang / contact shadow)
        mat = o["mat"]; above = np.roll(mat, 1, 0); dab = np.roll(o["depth"], 1, 0)
        edge = m & (above != WATER); edge[0] = False
        light_line = edge & ((above == MID) | (above == FAR) | ((above == GRASS) & (dab > 25)))
        dark_line = edge & ~light_line & ((above == GRASS) | (above == ROCK))
        self.img[light_line & (rng.uniform(size=(H, W)) < 0.8)] = self.pal.ids("sky")[5]
        self.img[dark_line & (rng.uniform(size=(H, W)) < 0.85)] = I("g_blk")
        # 4) back-lit: glitter path under the sun
        if sc.back:
            g = m & (tone(rf["col"]).sum(-1) > 2.6)
            self.img[g] = I("accent")

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
def paint_scene(size="medium", light="left", distance="10m", seed=0, return_all=False):
    sc = Scene(size, light, distance, seed)
    obs = observe(sc)
    pal = Palette(sc, obs)
    idx = Painter(sc, obs, pal).paint()
    rgb = (pal.rgb[idx] * 255).round().astype(np.uint8)
    if return_all: return rgb, (tone(obs["col"]) * 255).astype(np.uint8), pal, obs, sc
    return rgb

def up3(rgb): return np.repeat(np.repeat(rgb, 3, 0), 3, 1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("--size", default="medium"); ap.add_argument("--light", default="left")
    ap.add_argument("--distance", default="10m"); ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
    ap.add_argument("--out", default="out/single.png")
    a = ap.parse_args()
    rgb, obsimg, pal, obs, sc = paint_scene(a.size, a.light, a.distance, a.seed, True)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(a.out) or ".", exist_ok=True)
    Image.fromarray(up3(rgb)).save(a.out)
    Image.fromarray(up3(obsimg)).save(a.out.replace(".png", "_obs.png"))
    print("colours used:", len(np.unique(rgb.reshape(-1, 3), axis=0)), "palette:", len(pal.rgb))

"""print and draw the 32-colour palette of one parameter set (named swatches, L* values)"""
import sys, numpy as np
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
import tarn
def card(size, light, dist, seed, out):
    rgb, obsimg, pal, obs, sc = tarn.paint_scene(size, light, dist, seed, True)
    n = len(pal.rgb); im = Image.new("RGB", (8 * 90, ((n + 7) // 8) * 60), "white"); d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    for i, (nm, c) in enumerate(zip(pal.names, pal.rgb)):
        x, y = (i % 8) * 90, (i // 8) * 60
        d.rectangle([x, y, x + 88, y + 40], fill=tuple((c * 255).astype(int)))
        d.text((x + 2, y + 42), f"{nm} L{pal.lab[i][0]:.0f}", fill="black")
    im.save(out)
    for nm, c, l in zip(pal.names, pal.rgb, pal.lab): print(f"{nm:10s} {tuple((c*255).astype(int))}  L*={l[0]:.0f}")
if __name__ == "__main__":
    card(sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3], int(sys.argv[4]), sys.argv[5])

Bash: Rebalance palette values toward observed notan
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("tarn.py").read()
# sky: brighter, more chroma (Ferrari's sky sits at L*72-78, pink to violet)
s = s.replace("zen = np.array([0.30, 0.24, 0.48]); mid = np.array([0.62, 0.40, 0.58]); hor = np.array([0.95, 0.56, 0.48])",
              "zen = np.array([0.50, 0.36, 0.72]); mid = np.array([0.86, 0.50, 0.66]); hor = np.array([1.05, 0.58, 0.48])")
s = s.replace("return (base + glow + halo + belt) * 0.62 + disk", "return (base + glow + halo + belt) * 0.78 + disk")
# light: land darker than sky at dusk (notan: sky = light family)
s = s.replace("self.sunC = np.array([1.00, 0.56, 0.30]) * 3.0", "self.sunC = np.array([1.00, 0.52, 0.28]) * 2.3")
s = s.replace("self.rockA = np.array([0.46, 0.42, 0.38])", "self.rockA = np.array([0.50, 0.43, 0.38])")
# palette: grass families. lit = dry straw under warm sun; shadow = green-olive under violet sky
s = s.replace('''        gA = s.grassA; dry = gA * np.array([1.15, 1.0, 0.7]); green = gA * np.array([0.6, 1.0, 0.8])''',
'''        gA = s.grassA; dry = gA * np.array([1.15, 1.0, 0.7]); green = np.array([0.10, 0.20, 0.07]); olive = np.array([0.24, 0.26, 0.08])''')
s = s.replace('''        P["g_gap"] = tone(green * (sun * 0.03 + sky * 0.30))
        # grass shadow family (dark olive base / darker blade / near-black gap)
        P["g_dol"] = tone(gA * (sky * 0.95 + bnc * 0.3))
        P["g_dk"] = tone(gA * (sky * 0.45))
        P["g_blk"] = tone(gA * sky * 0.10)''', '''        P["g_gap"] = tone(green * (sun * 0.02 + sky * 0.25))
        # grass shadow family (dark olive base / darker blade / near-black gap)
        P["g_dol"] = tone(olive * (sky * 0.75 + bnc * 0.3))
        P["g_dk"] = tone(olive * (sky * 0.30))
        P["g_blk"] = tone(olive * sky * 0.05)''')
# rock: lit states lean on the sun (orange), shadows on the violet sky
s = s.replace('''        P["r_half"] = tone(rA * (sky * 1.05 + sun * 0.16))
        P["r_lit"] = tone(rA * (sky * 0.8 + sun * 0.62))
        P["r_rim"] = tone(rA * (sky * 0.8 + sun * 1.05))''', '''        P["r_half"] = tone(rA * (sky * 1.15 + sun * 0.12))
        P["r_lit"] = tone(rA * (sky * 0.5 + sun * 0.75))
        P["r_rim"] = tone(rA * (sky * 0.5 + sun * 1.2))''')
# sky ramp: evenly spaced along the elevation path (k-means wasted entries on near-duplicates)
s = s.replace('''        mat = obs["mat"]; skypx = obs["col"][mat == SKY]
        self.sky = kmeans_ramp(tone(np.concatenate([skypx, sk])), 8, s.seed)''', '''        self.sky = self.path_ramp(s, 8)''')
s = s.replace('''    @staticmethod
    def _lin(c):''', '''    @staticmethod
    def path_ramp(s, k):
        """sample the sky from zenith-ish (35 deg) down to the horizon at the view centre and toward
        the sun's side; pick k colours evenly spaced along the Lab path (distinct bands)."""
        fwd = s.basis()[0]; az0 = math.atan2(fwd[0], fwd[2])
        els = np.linspace(0.62, 0.0, 200)
        cols = []
        for daz in (-0.35, 0.0, 0.35):
            d = np.stack([np.sin(az0 + daz) * np.cos(els), np.sin(els), np.cos(az0 + daz) * np.cos(els)], -1)
            cols.append(tone(s.sky(d)))
        c = np.mean(cols, 0); lab = to_lab(c)
        seg = np.r_[0, np.cumsum(np.linalg.norm(np.diff(lab, axis=0), axis=1))]
        pick = [np.argmin(np.abs(seg - q)) for q in np.linspace(0, seg[-1], k)]
        out = c[pick]
        return out[np.argsort(to_lab(out)[:, 0])]

    @staticmethod
    def _lin(c):''')
open("tarn.py", "w").write(s)
EOF

Bash: Replace mountains with layered faceted pyramids
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("tarn.py").read()
start = s.index("    def mountain_ridge(self, az):")
end = s.index("    def mid_layer(self):")
new = '''    def peaks(self):
        """the range as layered pyramids (Ferrari's mountains are facets: each peak a lit flank and
        a shadow flank split at a leaning ridge line; flanks at ~45 deg and 2:1).
        rows: az, apex_el, slope_left, slope_right, ridge_lean, layer (0 far .. 2 near)"""
        if hasattr(self, "_peaks"): return self._peaks
        rng = np.random.default_rng(self.seed + 400)
        az0 = self.yaw; P = []
        for layer, n, el_rng in ((0, 6, (0.075, 0.12)), (1, 7, (0.05, 0.085)), (2, 8, (0.028, 0.055))):
            for _ in range(n):
                P.append((az0 + rng.uniform(-0.75, 0.75), rng.uniform(*el_rng), rng.uniform(0.55, 1.25),
                          rng.uniform(0.55, 1.25), rng.uniform(-0.5, 0.5), layer))
        self._peaks = np.array(P); return self._peaks

    def far_layer(self, d):
        """sky + distant granite range (dusk air), by direction -> linear rgb, mountain mask, lit mask"""
        az = np.arctan2(d[..., 0], d[..., 2]); el = np.arcsin(np.clip(d[..., 1], -1, 1))
        Pk = self.peaks()
        col = self.sky(d).copy(); mnt = np.zeros(az.shape, bool); lit = np.zeros(az.shape, bool)
        fwd, right, up = self.basis()
        sunx = self.sun @ right
        base_el = -0.01
        for layer in (0, 1, 2):                         # far to near: nearer layers occlude
            L = Pk[Pk[:, 5] == layer]
            best = np.full(az.shape, -np.inf); side = np.zeros(az.shape); snowline = np.zeros(az.shape)
            for pa, pe, sl, sr, lean, _ in L:
                dx = az - pa
                h = np.where(dx < 0, pe + sl * dx, pe - sr * dx)
                better = h > best
                ridge_x = pa + lean * (pe - el)            # the ridge line leans
                sd = np.where(az < ridge_x, -1.0, 1.0)
                best = np.where(better, h, best); side = np.where(better, sd, side)
                snowline = np.where(better, pe - 0.022 - 0.01 * layer, snowline)
            inside = (el < best) & (el > base_el)
            # flank normal: faces left or right, tilted up, toward the viewer
            vd = norm(np.stack([d[..., 0], np.zeros_like(el), d[..., 2]], -1))
            sidev = np.stack([vd[..., 2], np.zeros_like(el), -vd[..., 0]], -1)
            n = norm(-vd * 0.7 + sidev * side[..., None] * 0.9 + np.array([0, 1, 0]) * 0.55)
            ndl = np.clip(n @ self.sun, 0, 1)
            # snow: upper parts, ragged lower edge, more on shadow flanks (it survives there)
            rag = 0.006 * fbm2(az * 60, el * 60, self.seed + 44 + layer, 2)
            snow = (el > snowline + rag) & (fbm2(az * 25, el * 40, self.seed + 45, 2) > -0.35)
            alb = np.where(snow[..., None], np.array([0.78, 0.76, 0.86]), np.array([0.30, 0.26, 0.30]))
            c = alb * (self.sunC * 0.62 * ndl[..., None] + self.skyC * 1.25 * (0.55 + 0.45 * n[..., 1:2]))
            hz = self.sky(norm(np.stack([d[..., 0], np.full_like(el, 0.03), d[..., 2]], -1)))
            haze = (0.42, 0.3, 0.2)[layer]
            c = c * (1 - haze) + hz * haze
            col = np.where(inside[..., None], c, col); mnt |= inside
            lit = np.where(inside, ndl > 0.3, lit)
        return col, mnt, lit

'''
s = s[:start] + new + s[end:]
s = s.replace('''            rc[miss] = fc; rkey[miss] = np.where(midhit, mark[qy, qx], 0)''', '''            rc[miss] = fc; rkey[miss] = np.where(midhit, mark[qy, qx], 0)''')
# rock: brighter lit plateau (rough surface; painterly clarity)
s = s.replace("        lit = 3.75 + 1.25 * np.clip((lit_amt - 0.12) / 0.45, 0, 1) ** 0.7", "        lit = 3.8 + 1.3 * np.clip((lit_amt - 0.08) / 0.32, 0, 1) ** 0.6")
s = s.replace("        v = np.where(lit_amt > 0.12, np.maximum(lit, shd), shd)", "        v = np.where(lit_amt > 0.08, np.maximum(lit, shd), shd)")
s = s.replace("        for _ in range(int(len(ys_w) * 0.8) + 4):", "        for _ in range(int(len(ys_w) * 0.45) + 4):")
open("tarn.py", "w").write(s)

            for pa, pe, sl, sr, lean, _ in L:
                dx = az - pa

            jag = 0.010 * fbm2(az * 45 + layer * 7, np.full_like(az, 0.3), self.seed + 46 + layer, 3)

Write: pedagogy/observation/sheets.py
"""
sheets.py -- parameter sheets for the tarn method.  Every cell is a 320x180 painting shown at 3x
nearest-neighbour.  Four processes x one thread each (the 4-thread budget).

  python sheets.py            -> out/sheet_size.png, sheet_light.png, sheet_distance.png, sheet_seed.png
  python sheets.py light      -> just one
"""
import os, sys
os.environ["OMP_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"; os.environ["OPENBLAS_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"; os.environ["MKL_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
import numpy as np
from multiprocessing import Pool
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

BASE = dict(size="medium", light="left", distance="10m", seed=0)
SWEEPS = {
    "size": ("size", ["small", "medium", "large"], [1, 2, 3], dict(distance="10m")),
    "light": ("light", ["left", "front", "right", "back"], [1, 2, 3], {}),
    "distance": ("distance", ["close", "10m", "40m"], [1, 2, 3], {}),
    "seed": ("seed", [11, 12, 13, 14], [0, 1, 2], {}),
}

def render(args):
    import tarn
    kw, key = args
    os.makedirs("out/cells", exist_ok=True)
    fn = "out/cells/%s_%s_%s_s%d.png" % (kw["size"], kw["light"], kw["distance"], kw["seed"])
    if not os.path.exists(fn):
        rgb = tarn.paint_scene(**kw)
        Image.fromarray(rgb).save(fn)
    return key, fn

def make(name):
    param, values, seeds, extra = SWEEPS[name]
    jobs = []
    for r, v in enumerate(values):
        for c, sd in enumerate(seeds):
            kw = dict(BASE); kw.update(extra); kw[param] = v
            if name == "seed":        # seed sheet: rows = seeds, columns = a few light directions
                kw["seed"] = v; kw["light"] = ["left", "right", "back"][c]
            else:
                kw["seed"] = sd
            jobs.append((kw, (r, c)))
    with Pool(4) as p:
        res = p.map(render, jobs)
    S = 3; cw, ch = 320 * S, 180 * S; pad = 14; lab = 30
    rows, cols = len(values), len(seeds)
    sheet = Image.new("RGB", (cols * (cw + pad) + 150, rows * (ch + pad) + lab), (236, 232, 226))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(sheet)
    fixed = {k: v for k, v in dict(BASE, **extra).items() if k != param}
    title = f"sweep: {param}   (fixed: " + ", ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in fixed.items() if not (name != 'seed' and k == 'seed')) + ")"
    if name == "seed": title = "sweep: seed (rows)   columns: light = left / right / back;  size=medium, distance=10m"
    d.text((8, 8), title, fill=(40, 20, 20))
    for (r, c), fn in res:
        im = Image.open(fn).convert("RGB")
        im = im.resize((cw, ch), Image.NEAREST)
        sheet.paste(im, (150 + c * (cw + pad), lab + r * (ch + pad)))
    for r, v in enumerate(values):
        d.text((8, lab + r * (ch + pad) + ch // 2), f"{param} = {v}", fill=(40, 20, 20))
    if name != "seed":
        for c, sd in enumerate(seeds):
            d.text((150 + c * (cw + pad) + 4, lab - 14), f"seed {sd}", fill=(40, 20, 20))
    sheet.save(f"out/sheet_{name}.png"); print("wrote", name)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    names = sys.argv[1:] or list(SWEEPS)
    for n in names: make(n)

Bash: Write pixel statistics comparison script
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && cat > compare_stats.py <<'EOF'
"""pixel statistics that describe 'how it is painted', for Ferrari's tarn and my cells:
 colours per 8x8 tile (Ferrari's grass: 3-4 locally), isolated-pixel fraction (salt-and-pepper),
 mean horizontal / vertical run length, and total colours."""
import sys, glob, numpy as np
from PIL import Image
def stats(a):
    a = a.astype(np.int32); key = a[..., 0] * 65536 + a[..., 1] * 256 + a[..., 2]
    H, W = key.shape
    tiles = [len(np.unique(key[y:y + 8, x:x + 8])) for y in range(0, H - 7, 8) for x in range(0, W - 7, 8)]
    c = key[1:-1, 1:-1]
    iso = (c != key[:-2, 1:-1]) & (c != key[2:, 1:-1]) & (c != key[1:-1, :-2]) & (c != key[1:-1, 2:])
    def runs(k):
        L = []
        for r in k:
            ch = np.nonzero(r[1:] != r[:-1])[0]; L += list(np.diff(np.r_[-1, ch, len(r) - 1]))
        return np.mean(L)
    return dict(colours=len(np.unique(key)), tile_cols=np.mean(tiles), isolated=iso.mean(), hrun=runs(key), vrun=runs(key.T))
if __name__ == "__main__":
    f = np.array(Image.open("ferrari/native.png").convert("RGB"))
    print("%-40s" % "ferrari (native 634x469)", {k: round(float(v), 3) for k, v in stats(f).items()})
    for fn in sorted(glob.glob(sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "out/cells/*.png"))[:40]:
        a = np.array(Image.open(fn).convert("RGB"))
        print("%-40s" % fn.split("/")[-1], {k: round(float(v), 3) for k, v in stats(a).items()})
EOF

Bash: Grass lit model, layout variety, emergent sedge
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("tarn.py").read()
s = s.replace('''        t = np.clip(o["vis"] * (0.1 + 1.1 * o["ndl"]) / 0.75, 0, 1)''', '''        # grass is a volume of near-vertical blades: where the sun reaches it at all it is lit
        # (r23: lit vs shadow grass is decided by cast shadows, not by the ground's tilt); the
        # tussock slope facing the sun adds to it
        t = np.clip(o["vis"] * (0.45 + 1.2 * o["ndl"]), 0, 1)''')
s = s.replace("        ndc = 0.22 + rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05)", "        ndc = rng.uniform(0.12, 0.32)")
s = s.replace("        self.z1 = max(3.0, 0.3 * self.D)                   # near bank lip", "        self.z1 = 3.0 + 0.1 * self.D + rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.6)   # near bank lip")
s = s.replace('''def __init__(self, size="medium", light="left", distance="10m", seed=0, tree_density=0.5):''', '''def __init__(self, size="medium", light="left", distance="10m", seed=0, tree_density=None):''')
s = s.replace('''        self.tree_density = tree_density''', '''        self.tree_density = rng.uniform(0.3, 0.7) if tree_density is None else tree_density''')
# emergent sedge in the shallows (r19), painted after the water, before the bank grass
s = s.replace('''        self.paint_water()
        self.paint_rock()
        self.paint_grass()''', '''        self.paint_water()
        self.paint_sedge()
        self.paint_rock()
        self.paint_grass()''')
s = s.replace('''    # -- water (study C) -----------------------------------------------------------------------''', '''    # -- emergent sedge in the shallows (observed r19: blades standing out of the water near the
    #    lip, each with a darker, broken reflection hanging below its root) --------------------
    def paint_sedge(self):
        o = self.o; sc = self.sc; m = o["mat"] == WATER; rng = self.rng; I = self.I
        if not m.any(): return
        P = o["pos"]; bf = sc.bank_field(P[..., 0], P[..., 2])
        shallow = m & (bf > -0.9) & (bf < -0.08)
        clump = fbm2(P[..., 0] * 1.3, P[..., 2] * 1.3, sc.seed + 90, 2) > 0.25
        ys, xs = np.nonzero(shallow & clump & (rng.uniform(size=(H, W)) < 0.10))
        depth = o["depth"]; mat = o["mat"]; pix_ang = HFOV / W; img = self.img
        lit_side = not sc.back
        for y, x in zip(ys, xs):
            n = int(np.clip(0.45 / depth[y, x] / pix_ang * rng.uniform(0.5, 1.1), 2, 16))
            run = int(rng.choice([3, 4, 6, 0])); direc = -1 if rng.uniform() < 0.5 else 1
            body = I("g_dk"); tip = I("g_tan") if (lit_side and rng.uniform() < 0.55) else I("g_dol")
            xx = x; posr = 0
            for k in range(n):
                yy = y - k
                if yy < 0 or xx < 0 or xx >= W: break
                if depth[yy, xx] < depth[y, x] - 0.05: break
                img[yy, xx] = tip if k > n * 0.55 else body
                posr += 1
                if run and posr >= run: xx += direc; posr = 0
            # reflection: straight down, darker, broken by ripples
            for k in range(1, int(n * 0.8)):
                yy = y + k
                if yy >= H or not m[yy, x]: break
                if rng.uniform() < 0.75: img[yy, x] = I("g_blk") if k < n * 0.4 else I("g_dk")

    # -- water (study C) -----------------------------------------------------------------------''')
open("tarn.py", "w").write(s)
EOF

Bash: Test sun visibility from blade tops
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("tarn.py").read()
old = '''    def shade(self, p, mat, n, vdir):
        vis = self.soft_shadow(p, n); ao = self.ao(p, n)'''
new = '''    def shade(self, p, mat, n, vdir):
        # grass: test the sun from the blade tops (~0.2 m up), not the soil -- low sun still
        # reaches the tops of the grass on the far side of a hummock (lit tops, dark hollows)
        lift = np.where(mat == 2, 0.0, 0.2)[..., None] * np.array([0, 1.0, 0])
        vis = self.soft_shadow(p + lift, n); ao = self.ao(p, n)'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)
open("tarn.py", "w").write(s)
EOF

Bash: Add fall-line flecks to rock and preview
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("tarn.py").read()
old = '''        z[rim] = 6
        # contact: lowest boulder pixel in each column touching grass -> darkest'''
new = '''        z[rim] = 6
        # surface texture: short vertical flecks (1x2, 1x3) of the neighbouring value, running down
        # the fall line -- granite grain / weathering flutes. Ferrari's rock planes are never one
        # flat colour; each carries flecks of the next value. Density grows with size on screen.
        if npx > 250:
            dens = 0.05 + 0.07 * min(1.0, scale)
            seeds_ = m & (self.rng.uniform(size=(H, W)) < dens) & (z >= 3) & (z <= 5)
            ys_, xs_ = np.nonzero(seeds_)
            for y_, x_ in zip(ys_, xs_):
                zz = z[y_, x_]; nz = zz - 1 if (zz == 5 or self.rng.uniform() < 0.6) else zz + 1
                for k in range(int(self.rng.integers(1, 4))):
                    if y_ + k < H and m[y_ + k, x_] and z[y_ + k, x_] == zz: z[y_ + k, x_] = nz
        # contact: lowest boulder pixel in each column touching grass -> darkest'''
assert old in s
s = s.replace(old, new)
open("tarn.py", "w").write(s)
EOF

Bash: View-dependent grass lighting, back-lit tips, jaggier peaks
cd ~/work/pixelart-studies/pedagogy/observation && python3 - <<'EOF'
s = open("tarn.py").read()
old = '''        t = np.clip(o["vis"] * (0.45 + 1.2 * o["ndl"]), 0, 1)
        t = np.maximum(t, np.clip(o["trans"] * 1.3, 0, 1))
        return t'''
new = '''        t = np.clip(o["vis"] * (0.45 + 1.2 * o["ndl"]), 0, 1)
        # but we only see the lit side of a blade when the sun is not ahead of us: facing the sun,
        # the grass shows its shadowed side and only the tips glow (translucency, painted as tips)
        fwd = self.sc.basis()[0]; fh = np.array([fwd[0], 0, fwd[2]]); fh /= np.linalg.norm(fh)
        sh = np.array([self.sc.sun[0], 0, self.sc.sun[2]]); sh /= np.linalg.norm(sh)
        facing = np.clip(0.6 - 0.75 * float(fh @ sh), 0, 1)
        return t * facing'''
assert old in s; s = s.replace(old, new)
old2 = '''                if fam_lit:
                    c_body = I("g_ochre") if rng.uniform() < 0.75 else I("g_mid"); c_tip = I("g_tan") if rng.uniform() < 0.7 else I("g_ochre")
                    if t[y, x] > 0.85 and rng.uniform() < 0.4: c_tip = I("g_crest")
                else:
                    c_body = I("g_dk"); c_tip = I("g_dol")'''
new2 = '''                if fam_lit:
                    c_body = I("g_ochre") if rng.uniform() < 0.75 else I("g_mid"); c_tip = I("g_tan") if rng.uniform() < 0.7 else I("g_ochre")
                    if t[y, x] > 0.85 and rng.uniform() < 0.4: c_tip = I("g_crest")
                else:
                    c_body = I("g_dk"); c_tip = I("g_dol")
                    if o["trans"][y, x] > 0.25 and rng.uniform() < 0.7:      # back-lit: glowing tips
                        c_tip = I("g_crest") if rng.uniform() < 0.4 else I("g_tan")'''
assert old2 in s; s = s.replace(old2, new2)
s = s.replace("            jag = 0.010 * fbm2(az * 45 + layer * 7, np.full_like(az, 0.3), self.seed + 46 + layer, 3)",
              "            jag = 0.016 * fbm2(az * 40 + layer * 7, np.full_like(az, 0.3), self.seed + 46 + layer, 4)")
s = s.replace("        for layer, n, el_rng in ((0, 6, (0.075, 0.12)), (1, 7, (0.05, 0.085)), (2, 8, (0.028, 0.055))):",
              "        for layer, n, el_rng in ((0, 9, (0.07, 0.12)), (1, 10, (0.045, 0.085)), (2, 11, (0.025, 0.055))):")
open("tarn.py", "w").write(s)
